import { useMemo, useState } from "react";

import {
    Cake,
    CalendarDays,
    ChevronLeft,
    ChevronRight,
    Eye,
    Gift,
} from "lucide-react";

import "../../../styles/components/administration/members/MembersBirthdays.css";
import MembersPagination from "./MembersPagination";

// =========================================================
// CONFIGURACIÓN
// =========================================================

const MONTHS = [
    "enero", "febrero", "marzo", "abril",
    "mayo", "junio", "julio", "agosto",
    "septiembre", "octubre", "noviembre",
    "diciembre",
];

// =========================================================
// FUNCIONES AUXILIARES
// =========================================================

function getInitials(name = "") {
    return name
        .trim()
        .split(/\s+/)
        .slice(0, 2)
        .map((part) => part.charAt(0).toUpperCase())
        .join("");
}

function getBirthdayParts(birthDate) {
    if (!birthDate) return null;

    const parts = birthDate.split("-").map(Number);

    if (
        parts.length !== 3 ||
        parts.some((part) => !Number.isInteger(part))
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    const [year, month, day] = parts;
    const date = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));

    if (
        date.getUTCFullYear() !== year ||
        date.getUTCMonth() !== month - 1 ||
        date.getUTCDate() !== day
    ) {
        return null;
    }

    return { month: month - 1, day };
}

// =========================================================
// COMPONENTE PRINCIPAL
// =========================================================

function MembersBirthdays({ members = [], onViewMember }) {

    // Mes actual como valor inicial.
    const today = new Date();

    const [selectedMonth, setSelectedMonth] =
        useState(today.getMonth());

    // =====================================================
    // PAGINACIÓN DE CUMPLEAÑOS
    // =====================================================

    const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);

    const [pageSize, setPageSize] = useState(6);

    const currentMonth = today.getMonth();
    const currentDay = today.getDate();

    // =====================================================
    // CUMPLEAÑOS DEL MES
    // =====================================================

    const birthdays = useMemo(() => {
        return members
            .filter((member) => {
                const birthday = getBirthdayParts(member.birthDate);

                return birthday?.month === selectedMonth;
            })
            .sort((a, b) => {
                const dayA = getBirthdayParts(a.birthDate).day;
                const dayB = getBirthdayParts(b.birthDate).day;

                return dayA - dayB ||
                    a.name.localeCompare(b.name, "es");
            });
    }, [members, selectedMonth]);

    // =====================================================
    // CUMPLEAÑOS DE LA PÁGINA ACTUAL
    // =====================================================

    const paginatedBirthdays = useMemo(() => {

        const startIndex = (currentPage - 1) * pageSize;

        return birthdays.slice(
            startIndex,
            startIndex + pageSize
        );

    }, [birthdays, currentPage, pageSize]);

    // =====================================================
    // CAMBIAR MES
    // =====================================================

    function handlePreviousMonth() {
        setSelectedMonth((previous) => (previous + 11) % 12);
        setCurrentPage(1);
    }

    function handleNextMonth() {
        setSelectedMonth((previous) => (previous + 1) % 12);
        setCurrentPage(1);
    }

    // =====================================================
    // CONTROL DE PAGINACIÓN
    // =====================================================

    function handlePageChange(page) {

        const totalPages = Math.max(
            1,
            Math.ceil(birthdays.length / pageSize)
        );

        if (page >= 1 && page <= totalPages) {
            setCurrentPage(page);
        }
    }

    function handlePageSizeChange(size) {
        setPageSize(size);
        setCurrentPage(1);
    }

    // =====================================================
    // ETIQUETA DE CUMPLEAÑOS
    // =====================================================

    function getBirthdayStatus(day) {

        if (selectedMonth !== currentMonth) {
            return null;
        }

        if (day === currentDay) {
            return {
                label: "¡Hoy!",
                className: "today",
            };
        }

        if (day > currentDay) {
            return {
                label: "Próximo",
                className: "upcoming",
            };
        }

        return {
            label: "Ya pasó",
            className: "past",
        };
    }

    // =====================================================
    // INTERFAZ
    // =====================================================

    return (
        <section className="members-birthdays">

            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* ENCABEZADO */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            <div className="members-birthdays__header">

                <div className="members-birthdays__heading">
                    <div className="members-birthdays__icon">
                        <Cake size={22} aria-hidden="true" />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <h2>Cumpleaños de la comunidad</h2>

                        <p>
                            Conoce las fechas especiales
                            de nuestros miembros.
                        </p>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <span className="members-birthdays__count">
                    {birthdays.length} cumpleaños
                </span>

            </div>

            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* SELECTOR DE MES */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            <div className="members-birthdays__month-navigation">

                <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handlePreviousMonth}
                    aria-label="Mes anterior"
                >
                    <ChevronLeft size={19} />
                </button>

                <div className="members-birthdays__month-title">
                    <CalendarDays size={17} aria-hidden="true" />

                    <span>
                        {MONTHS[selectedMonth]}
                    </span>
                </div>

                <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleNextMonth}
                    aria-label="Mes siguiente"
                >
                    <ChevronRight size={19} />
                </button>

            </div>

            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* LISTADO DE CUMPLEAÑOS */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            {birthdays.length > 0 ? (

                <div className="members-birthdays__list">

                    {paginatedBirthdays.map((member) => {

                        const birthday =
                            getBirthdayParts(member.birthDate);

                        const status =
                            getBirthdayStatus(birthday.day);

                        return (
                            <article
                                className="members-birthdays__member"
                                key={member.id}
                            >

                                <div className="members-birthdays__avatar">
                                    {getInitials(member.name)}
                                </div>

                                <div className="members-birthdays__member-info">

                                    <strong>{member.name}</strong>

                                    <span>
                                        <Gift
                                            size={14}
                                            aria-hidden="true"
                                        />

                                        {birthday.day} de{" "}
                                        {MONTHS[selectedMonth]}
                                    </span>

                                </div>

                                {status && (
                                    <span
                                        className={`members-birthdays__status members-birthdays__status--${status.className}`}
                                    >
                                        {status.label}
                                    </span>
                                )}

                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    className="members-birthdays__view"
                                    onClick={() =>
                                        onViewMember?.(member)
                                    }
                                    aria-label={`Ver perfil de ${member.name}`}
                                >
                                    <Eye
                                        size={17}
                                        aria-hidden="true"
                                    />
                                </button>

                            </article>
                        );
                    })}

                </div>

            ) : (

                <div className="members-birthdays__empty">

                    <Cake
                        size={33}
                        strokeWidth={1.5}
                        aria-hidden="true"
                    />

                    <p>
                        No hay cumpleaños registrados
                        durante este mes.
                    </p>

                </div>

            )}

            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* PAGINACIÓN DE CUMPLEAÑOS */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            {birthdays.length > 0 && (
                <MembersPagination
                    currentPage={currentPage}
                    totalItems={birthdays.length}
                    pageSize={pageSize}
                    onPageChange={handlePageChange}
                    onPageSizeChange={handlePageSizeChange}
                    pageSizeOptions={[6, 12, 24]}
                    itemLabel="cumpleaños"
                />
            )}

        </section>
    );
}

export default MembersBirthdays;

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";

import {
    ArrowRight,
    CalendarDays,
    Eye,
    Mail,
    Search,
    UsersRound,
} from "lucide-react";

import MembersPagination from "./MembersPagination";
import "../../../styles/components/administration/members/MembersDirectory.css";

// =========================================================
// CONFIGURACIÓN DE ESTADOS
// =========================================================

const STATUS_CONFIG = {
    active: {
        label: "Activo",
        className: "active",
    },
    blocked: {
        label: "Bloqueado",
        className: "blocked",
    },
};

// =========================================================
// FUNCIONES AUXILIARES
// =========================================================

function getInitials(name = "") {
    return name
        .trim()
        .split(/\s+/)
        .slice(0, 2)
        .map((part) => part.charAt(0).toUpperCase())
        .join("");
}

function formatDate(value) {
    if (!value) return "No informado";

    const date = new Date(`${value}T12:00:00Z`);

    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
        return "Fecha no disponible";
    }

    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-CL", {
        day: "2-digit",
        month: "long",
        year: "numeric",
        timeZone: "UTC",
    }).format(date);
}

// =========================================================
// COMPONENTE PRINCIPAL
// =========================================================

function MembersDirectory({
    members = [],
    onViewMember,
}) {

    // =====================================================
    // FILTROS
    // =====================================================

    const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
    const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("all");

    // =====================================================
    // PAGINACIÓN
    // =====================================================

    const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);
    const [pageSize, setPageSize] = useState(10);

    // =====================================================
    // FILTRAR MIEMBROS
    // =====================================================

    const filteredMembers = useMemo(() => {

        const normalizedSearch = search.trim().toLowerCase();

        return members.filter((member) => {

            const matchesSearch =
                !normalizedSearch ||
                (member.name || "")
                    .toLowerCase()
                    .includes(normalizedSearch) ||
                (member.email || "")
                    .toLowerCase()
                    .includes(normalizedSearch);

            const matchesStatus =
                statusFilter === "all" ||
                member.status === statusFilter;

            return matchesSearch && matchesStatus;
        });

    }, [members, search, statusFilter]);

    // =====================================================
    // PAGINAR RESULTADOS
    // =====================================================

    const totalPages = Math.max(
        1,
        Math.ceil(filteredMembers.length / pageSize)
    );

    // Si la lista cambia, evitamos utilizar una página
    // superior a la cantidad de páginas disponibles.
    const safeCurrentPage = Math.min(
        currentPage,
        totalPages
    );

    const paginatedMembers = useMemo(() => {

        const startIndex =
            (safeCurrentPage - 1) * pageSize;

        return filteredMembers.slice(
            startIndex,
            startIndex + pageSize
        );

    }, [filteredMembers, safeCurrentPage, pageSize]);

    // =====================================================
    // CONTROL DE FILTROS
    // =====================================================

    function handleSearchChange(event) {
        setSearch(event.target.value);
        setCurrentPage(1);
    }

    function handleStatusChange(event) {
        setStatusFilter(event.target.value);
        setCurrentPage(1);
    }

    function handleClearFilters() {
        setSearch("");
        setStatusFilter("all");
        setCurrentPage(1);
    }

    const hasActiveFilters =
        search.trim() !== "" ||
        statusFilter !== "all";

    // =====================================================
    // CONTROL DE PAGINACIÓN
    // =====================================================

    function handlePageChange(page) {
        if (page >= 1 && page <= totalPages) {
            setCurrentPage(page);
        }
    }

    function handlePageSizeChange(size) {
        setPageSize(size);
        setCurrentPage(1);
    }

    // =====================================================
    // INTERFAZ
    // =====================================================

    return (
        <section className="members-management__content">

            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* ENCABEZADO DEL DIRECTORIO */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            <div className="members-management__section-heading">

                <div>
                    <h2>Directorio de miembros</h2>

                    <p>
                        Consulta los perfiles de los miembros
                        registrados en Iglesia El Ancla.
                    </p>
                </div>

                <span className="members-management__count">
                    {filteredMembers.length} resultados
                </span>

            </div>

            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* FILTROS */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            <div className="members-management__filters">

                <label className="members-management__search">

                    <Search
                        size={18}
                        aria-hidden="true"
                    />

                    <input
                        type="search"
                        value={search}
                        onChange={handleSearchChange}
                        placeholder="Buscar por nombre o correo..."
                        aria-label="Buscar miembros"
                    />

                </label>

                <select
                    aria-label="Filtrar miembros por estado"
                    value={statusFilter}
                    onChange={handleStatusChange}
                >
                    <option value="all">
                        Todos los estados
                    </option>

                    <option value="active">
                        Activos
                    </option>

                    <option value="blocked">
                        Bloqueados
                    </option>
                </select>

                {hasActiveFilters && (
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        className="members-management__clear"
                        onClick={handleClearFilters}
                    >
                        Limpiar filtros
                    </button>
                )}

            </div>

            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* LISTADO */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            <div className="members-management__list">

                {paginatedMembers.map((member) => {

                    const status =
                        STATUS_CONFIG[member.status] || {
                            label: "Sin estado",
                            className: "unknown",
                        };

                    return (
                        <article
                            className="members-management__member"
                            key={member.id}
                        >

                            <div className="members-management__avatar">
                                {getInitials(member.name)}
                            </div>

                            <div className="members-management__member-info">

                                <strong>{member.name}</strong>

                                <span>
                                    <Mail
                                        size={14}
                                        aria-hidden="true"
                                    />

                                    {member.email}
                                </span>

                                <small>
                                    <CalendarDays
                                        size={13}
                                        aria-hidden="true"
                                    />

                                    Registrado:{" "}
                                    {formatDate(member.registeredAt)}
                                </small>

                            </div>

                            <div className="members-management__member-status">

                                <span
                                    className={
                                        `members-management__status ` +
                                        `members-management__status--${status.className}`
                                    }
                                >
                                    {status.label}
                                </span>

                            </div>

                            <button
                                type="button"
                                className="members-management__details-button"
                                onClick={() =>
                                    onViewMember?.(member)
                                }
                            >
                                <Eye
                                    size={16}
                                    aria-hidden="true"
                                />

                                Ver perfil

                                <ArrowRight
                                    size={15}
                                    aria-hidden="true"
                                />
                            </button>

                        </article>
                    );
                })}

                {/* ======================================= */}
                {/* ESTADO VACÍO */}
                {/* ======================================= */}

                {filteredMembers.length === 0 && (

                    <div className="members-management__empty">

                        <UsersRound
                            size={35}
                            strokeWidth={1.5}
                            aria-hidden="true"
                        />

                        <h3>No encontramos miembros</h3>

                        <p>
                            {hasActiveFilters
                                ? "Prueba con otro nombre o modifica los filtros seleccionados."
                                : "Todavía no hay miembros registrados en el sistema."}
                        </p>

                    </div>
                )}

            </div>

            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* PAGINACIÓN */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            {filteredMembers.length > 0 && (

                <MembersPagination
                    currentPage={safeCurrentPage}
                    totalItems={filteredMembers.length}
                    pageSize={pageSize}
                    onPageChange={handlePageChange}
                    onPageSizeChange={handlePageSizeChange}
                />

            )}

        </section>
    );
}

export default MembersDirectory;

import {
    ChevronLeft,
    ChevronRight,
} from "lucide-react";

import "../../../styles/components/administration/members/MembersPagination.css";

// =========================================================
// COMPONENTE DE PAGINACIÓN
// =========================================================

function MembersPagination({
    currentPage,
    totalItems,
    pageSize,
    onPageChange,
    onPageSizeChange,
    pageSizeOptions = [10, 20, 50],
    itemLabel = "miembros",
}) {

    // =====================================================
    // CÁLCULOS
    // =====================================================

    const totalPages = Math.max(
        1,
        Math.ceil(totalItems / pageSize)
    );

    const startItem =
        totalItems === 0
            ? 0
            : (currentPage - 1) * pageSize + 1;

    const endItem = Math.min(
        currentPage * pageSize,
        totalItems
    );

    const canGoPrevious = currentPage > 1;
    const canGoNext = currentPage < totalPages;

    // =====================================================
    // GENERAR NÚMEROS DE PÁGINA
    // =====================================================

    function getVisiblePages() {

        const pages = [];

        for (let page = 1; page <= totalPages; page++) {

            const isFirst = page === 1;
            const isLast = page === totalPages;

            const isNearCurrent =
                Math.abs(page - currentPage) <= 1;

            if (isFirst || isLast || isNearCurrent) {
                pages.push(page);
            }
        }

        // Insertamos separadores cuando hay saltos.
        const result = [];

        pages.forEach((page, index) => {

            if (index > 0) {

                const previous = pages[index - 1];

                if (page - previous > 1) {
                    result.push(`ellipsis-${previous}`);
                }
            }

            result.push(page);
        });

        return result;
    }

    const visiblePages = getVisiblePages();

    // =====================================================
    // INTERFAZ
    // =====================================================

    return (
        <nav
            className="members-pagination"
            aria-label="Paginación del directorio"
        >

            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* INFORMACIÓN DE RESULTADOS */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            <div className="members-pagination__information">

                <span>
                    Mostrando{" "}
                    <strong>{startItem}–{endItem}</strong>
                    {" "}de{" "}
                    <strong>{totalItems}</strong>
                    {" "}{itemLabel}
                </span>

            </div>

            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* CONTROLES */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            <div className="members-pagination__controls">

                {/* CANTIDAD POR PÁGINA */}

                <label className="members-pagination__page-size">

                    <span>Mostrar</span>

                    <select
                        value={pageSize}
                        onChange={(event) =>
                            onPageSizeChange(
                                Number(event.target.value)
                            )
                        }
                        aria-label="Miembros por página"
                    >
                        {pageSizeOptions.map((size) => (
                            <option key={size} value={size}>
                                {size}
                            </option>
                        ))}
                    </select>

                </label>

                {/* BOTONES DE NAVEGACIÓN */}

                <div className="members-pagination__buttons">

                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() =>
                            onPageChange(currentPage - 1)
                        }
                        disabled={!canGoPrevious}
                        aria-label="Página anterior"
                    >
                        <ChevronLeft
                            size={17}
                            aria-hidden="true"
                        />
                    </button>

                    {visiblePages.map((page) => {

                        if (typeof page === "string") {
                            return (
                                <span
                                    className="members-pagination__ellipsis"
                                    key={page}
                                >
                                    …
                                </span>
                            );
                        }

                        return (
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                key={page}
                                className={
                                    currentPage === page
                                        ? "members-pagination__button--active"
                                        : ""
                                }
                                onClick={() => onPageChange(page)}
                                aria-label={`Página ${page}`}
                                aria-current={
                                    currentPage === page
                                        ? "page"
                                        : undefined
                                }
                            >
                                {page}
                            </button>
                        );
                    })}

                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() =>
                            onPageChange(currentPage + 1)
                        }
                        disabled={!canGoNext}
                        aria-label="Página siguiente"
                    >
                        <ChevronRight
                            size={17}
                            aria-hidden="true"
                        />
                    </button>

                </div>

            </div>

        </nav>
    );
}

export default MembersPagination;

import { useEffect, useId, useState } from "react";

import {
    CalendarDays,
    CheckCircle2,
    Clock3,
    HandHeart,
    LockKeyhole,
    MessageSquareText,
    Save,
    ShieldCheck,
    UserRound,
    X,
} from "lucide-react";

import "../../../styles/components/administration/prayers/PrayerRequestDetailsModal.css";

// =========================================================
// CONFIGURACIÓN DE ESTADOS
// =========================================================

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
    {
        value: "pending",
        label: "Pendiente",
    },
    {
        value: "praying",
        label: "En oración",
    },
    {
        value: "answered",
        label: "Respondida",
    },
    {
        value: "completed",
        label: "Finalizada",
    },
];

// =========================================================
// FORMATO DE FECHA
// =========================================================

function formatPrayerDate(value) {
    if (!value) return "Sin fecha";

    const date = new Date(`${value}T12:00:00Z`);

    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
        return "Fecha no disponible";
    }

    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-CL", {
        day: "2-digit",
        month: "long",
        year: "numeric",
        timeZone: "UTC",
    }).format(date);
}

// =========================================================
// COMPONENTE
// =========================================================

function PrayerRequestDetailsModal({
    isOpen,
    request,
    onClose,
    onSave,
}) {
    const titleId = useId();

    const [status, setStatus] = useState("pending");
    const [followUpNote, setFollowUpNote] = useState("");

    const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);

    // -------------------------------------------------------
    // CARGAR DATOS DE LA PETICIÓN SELECCIONADA
    // -------------------------------------------------------

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isOpen || !request) return;

        setStatus(request.status || "pending");
        setFollowUpNote(request.followUpNote || "");
        setIsSaving(false);
    }, [isOpen, request]);

    // -------------------------------------------------------
    // CERRAR CON ESCAPE
    // -------------------------------------------------------

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isOpen) return;

        function handleKeyDown(event) {
            if (event.key === "Escape") {
                onClose();
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);

        return () => {
            document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
        };
    }, [isOpen, onClose]);

    // =========================================================
    // GUARDAR CAMBIOS
    // =========================================================

    async function handleSubmit(event) {
        event.preventDefault();

        // Evitamos solicitudes duplicadas.
        if (!request || isSaving) {
            return;
        }

        setIsSaving(true);

        try {

            // Entregamos los cambios a la página principal.
            //
            // Actualmente se actualizan datos demostrativos.
            // Posteriormente onSave podrá ejecutar
            // una solicitud HTTP al backend.
            await onSave(request.id, {
                status,
                followUpNote: followUpNote.trim(),
            });

        } catch (error) {

            // Si ocurre un error, lo registramos.
            // Más adelante mostraremos un mensaje
            // visual de error dentro del modal.
            console.error(
                "No fue posible guardar el seguimiento:",
                error
            );

        } finally {

            setIsSaving(false);

        }
    }

    if (!isOpen || !request) {
        return null;
    }

    const isPrivate = request.privacy === "private";

    // Los administradores siempre pueden identificar
    // al miembro que realizó la petición.
    //
    // El anonimato solamente oculta su identidad
    // en las vistas destinadas a otros miembros.

    const requesterName =
        request.requester || "No informado";

    const hasChanges =
        status !== request.status ||
        followUpNote.trim() !== (request.followUpNote || "");

    return (
        <div
            className="prayer-details__overlay"
            onMouseDown={(event) => {
                if (event.target === event.currentTarget) {
                    onClose();
                }
            }}
        >
            <section
                className="prayer-details"
                role="dialog"
                aria-modal="true"
                aria-labelledby={titleId}
            >
                {/* =========================================== */}
                {/* ENCABEZADO */}
                {/* =========================================== */}

                <header className="prayer-details__header">
                    <div>
                        <span className="prayer-details__eyebrow">
                            {request.id} · PETICIÓN DE ORACIÓN
                        </span>

                        <h2 id={titleId}>
                            Detalle de petición
                        </h2>

                        <p>
                            Consulta la solicitud y registra su seguimiento.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <button
                        type="button"
                        className="prayer-details__close"
                        onClick={onClose}
                        aria-label="Cerrar detalles"
                    >
                        <X size={20} aria-hidden="true" />
                    </button>
                </header>

                {/* =========================================== */}
                {/* CONTENIDO */}
                {/* =========================================== */}

                <div className="prayer-details__content">

                    <div className="prayer-details__badges">
                        <span
                            className={`prayer-details__privacy ${isPrivate
                                ? "prayer-details__privacy--private"
                                : "prayer-details__privacy--community"
                                }`}
                        >
                            {isPrivate ? (
                                <LockKeyhole size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
                            ) : (
                                <HandHeart size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
                            )}

                            {isPrivate ? "Privada" : "Comunitaria"}
                        </span>

                        {request.anonymous && (
                            <span className="prayer-details__anonymous">
                                <ShieldCheck size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
                                Anónima para miembros
                            </span>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    {/* ========================================= */}
                    {/* INFORMACIÓN GENERAL */}
                    {/* ========================================= */}

                    <div className="prayer-details__information">

                        <div className="prayer-details__field prayer-details__field--wide">
                            <span>Título de la petición</span>
                            <strong>{request.title}</strong>
                        </div>

                        {/* IDENTIDAD DEL SOLICITANTE */}

                        <div className="prayer-details__field">
                            <span>
                                <UserRound size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
                                Solicitante registrado
                            </span>

                            <strong>{requesterName}</strong>

                            {request.anonymous && (
                                <small>
                                    Identidad visible únicamente para
                                    administradores autorizados.
                                </small>
                            )}
                        </div>

                        <div className="prayer-details__field">
                            <span>
                                <CalendarDays size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
                                Fecha de recepción
                            </span>
                            <strong>{formatPrayerDate(request.date)}</strong>
                        </div>

                        <div className="prayer-details__field">
                            <span>Categoría</span>
                            <strong>{request.category}</strong>
                        </div>

                        <div className="prayer-details__field">
                            <span>
                                <Clock3 size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
                                Estado actual
                            </span>

                            <strong>
                                {STATUS_OPTIONS.find(
                                    (option) => option.value === request.status
                                )?.label || "Pendiente"}
                            </strong>
                        </div>

                    </div>

                    {/* ========================================= */}
                    {/* CONTENIDO DE LA PETICIÓN */}
                    {/* ========================================= */}

                    <div className="prayer-details__message">

                        <h3>
                            <MessageSquareText
                                size={19}
                                aria-hidden="true"
                            />
                            Contenido de la petición
                        </h3>

                        <p>
                            {request.description ||
                                "Esta petición no contiene una descripción."}
                        </p>

                    </div>

                    {/* ========================================= */}
                    {/* FORMULARIO DE SEGUIMIENTO */}
                    {/* ========================================= */}

                    <form
                        id="prayer-details-form"
                        onSubmit={handleSubmit}
                        className="prayer-details__form"
                    >
                        <h3>
                            <CheckCircle2 size={19} aria-hidden="true" />
                            Seguimiento de la petición
                        </h3>

                        <div className="prayer-details__form-field">

                            <label htmlFor="prayer-details-status">
                                Estado de seguimiento
                            </label>

                            <select
                                id="prayer-details-status"
                                value={status}
                                onChange={(event) =>
                                    setStatus(event.target.value)
                                }
                            >
                                {STATUS_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
                                    <option
                                        key={option.value}
                                        value={option.value}
                                    >
                                        {option.label}
                                    </option>
                                ))}
                            </select>

                        </div>

                        <div className="prayer-details__form-field">

                            <label htmlFor="prayer-details-note">
                                Observación de seguimiento
                            </label>

                            <textarea
                                id="prayer-details-note"
                                rows={4}
                                maxLength={1000}
                                value={followUpNote}
                                onChange={(event) =>
                                    setFollowUpNote(event.target.value)
                                }
                                placeholder="Registra una observación breve sobre el seguimiento realizado..."
                            />

                            <small>
                                Esta observación será de uso interno y no
                                deberá mostrarse públicamente.
                            </small>

                        </div>

                    </form>

                    {/* ========================================= */}
                    {/* AVISO DE CONFIDENCIALIDAD */}
                    {/* ========================================= */}

                    <div className="prayer-details__notice">
                        <ShieldCheck size={18} aria-hidden="true" />

                        <p>
                            Esta información es confidencial y debe utilizarse
                            exclusivamente para el acompañamiento espiritual
                            y seguimiento autorizado. Las peticiones privadas
                            no deben compartirse con otros miembros y la
                            identidad de quienes soliciten anonimato debe
                            permanecer protegida fuera del equipo administrativo.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                </div>

                {/* =========================================== */}
                {/* PIE DEL MODAL */}
                {/* =========================================== */}

                <footer className="prayer-details__footer">

                    <button
                        type="button"
                        className="prayer-details__cancel"
                        onClick={onClose}
                    >
                        Cerrar
                    </button>

                    <button
                        type="submit"
                        form="prayer-details-form"
                        className="prayer-details__save"
                        disabled={!hasChanges || isSaving}
                    >
                        <Save size={17} aria-hidden="true" />
                        Guardar cambios
                    </button>

                </footer>

            </section>
        </div>
    );
}

export default PrayerRequestDetailsModal;

import { useState } from "react";

import {
  UserRound,
  Mail,
  Phone,
  CalendarDays,
  ShieldCheck,
  Building2,
  ClipboardCheck,
  RotateCcw,
  UserPlus,
  Info,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertCircle,
} from "lucide-react";

import "../../../styles/components/administration/users/RegisterUserForm.css";


// =========================================================
// DATOS DE DEMOSTRACIÓN
// =========================================================

// Estas sucursales son temporales.
// Posteriormente las obtendremos desde MongoDB.

const DEMO_BRANCHES = [
  {
    id: "demo-suc-001",
    code: "SUC-001",
    name: "El Ancla - Playa Ancha",
  },
];


// =========================================================
// FORMULARIO INICIAL
// =========================================================

const EMPTY_FORM = {
  firstName: "",
  lastName: "",
  email: "",
  phone: "",
  birthDate: "",
  role: "member",
  sucursales: ["demo-suc-001"],
  sucursalPrincipal: "demo-suc-001",
};


// =========================================================
// FUNCIONES AUXILIARES
// =========================================================

const getRoleLabel = (role) => {
  return role === "admin"
    ? "Administrador"
    : "Miembro";
};


const validateForm = (values) => {

  const errors = {};

  const firstName = values.firstName.trim();
  const lastName = values.lastName.trim();
  const email = values.email.trim();
  const phone = values.phone.trim();

  if (firstName.length < 2 || firstName.length > 60) {
    errors.firstName =
      "Los nombres deben tener entre 2 y 60 caracteres.";
  }

  if (lastName.length < 2 || lastName.length > 60) {
    errors.lastName =
      "Los apellidos deben tener entre 2 y 60 caracteres.";
  }

  if (
    !email ||
    email.length > 150 ||
    !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)
  ) {
    errors.email =
      "Ingresa un correo electrónico válido.";
  }

  if (
    !phone ||
    phone.length > 30 ||
    !/^\+?[0-9\s()-]{8,30}$/.test(phone)
  ) {
    errors.phone =
      "Ingresa un teléfono válido.";
  }

  if (!values.birthDate) {

    errors.birthDate =
      "La fecha de nacimiento es obligatoria.";

  } else {

    const date = new Date(
      `${values.birthDate}T00:00:00Z`
    );

    const isValidDate =
      !Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) &&
      date.toISOString().slice(0, 10) ===
        values.birthDate;

    if (!isValidDate || date > new Date()) {
      errors.birthDate =
        "La fecha de nacimiento no es válida.";
    }

  }

  if (!["member", "admin"].includes(values.role)) {
    errors.role = "Selecciona un rol válido.";
  }

  if (values.sucursales.length === 0) {
    errors.sucursales =
      "Selecciona al menos una sucursal.";
  }

  if (
    !values.sucursalPrincipal ||
    !values.sucursales.includes(
      values.sucursalPrincipal
    )
  ) {
    errors.sucursalPrincipal =
      "Selecciona una sucursal principal válida.";
  }

  return errors;
};


// =========================================================
// COMPONENTE PRINCIPAL
// =========================================================

function RegisterUserForm({
  mode = "super_admin",
  branches = DEMO_BRANCHES,
}) {

  // =========================================================
  // ESTADOS
  // =========================================================

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    ...EMPTY_FORM,
    sucursales: [...EMPTY_FORM.sucursales],
  });

  const [errors, setErrors] = useState({});

  const [showConfirmation, setShowConfirmation] =
    useState(false);

  const [showDemoSuccess, setShowDemoSuccess] =
    useState(false);


  // =========================================================
  // PERMISOS VISUALES
  // =========================================================

  // En el futuro estos permisos también deberán
  // comprobarse en el backend.

  const isSuperAdmin = mode === "super_admin";

  const availableRoles = isSuperAdmin
    ? [
        { value: "member", label: "Miembro" },
        { value: "admin", label: "Administrador" },
      ]
    : [
        { value: "member", label: "Miembro" },
      ];


  // =========================================================
  // INFORMACIÓN PARA LA VISTA PREVIA
  // =========================================================

  const fullName = [
    formData.firstName.trim(),
    formData.lastName.trim(),
  ].filter(Boolean).join(" ");

  const initials = fullName
    ? fullName
        .split(/\s+/)
        .slice(0, 2)
        .map((part) => part[0]?.toUpperCase())
        .join("")
    : "EA";

  const mainBranch = branches.find(
    (branch) =>
      String(branch.id || branch._id) ===
      formData.sucursalPrincipal
  );

  const hasErrors =
    Object.keys(errors).length > 0;


  // =========================================================
  // ACTUALIZAR CAMPOS
  // =========================================================

  const handleChange = (event) => {

    const { name, value } = event.target;

    setFormData((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      [name]: value,
    }));

    setErrors((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      [name]: undefined,
    }));

    setShowConfirmation(false);
    setShowDemoSuccess(false);
  };


  // =========================================================
  // SELECCIONAR SUCURSALES
  // =========================================================

  const handleBranchChange = (branchId, checked) => {

    setFormData((previous) => {

      const selected = checked
        ? [...new Set([...previous.sucursales, branchId])]
        : previous.sucursales.filter(
            (id) => id !== branchId
          );

      const mainBranchId =
        selected.includes(previous.sucursalPrincipal)
          ? previous.sucursalPrincipal
          : selected[0] || "";

      return {
        ...previous,
        sucursales: selected,
        sucursalPrincipal: mainBranchId,
      };

    });

    setErrors((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      sucursales: undefined,
      sucursalPrincipal: undefined,
    }));

    setShowConfirmation(false);
    setShowDemoSuccess(false);
  };


  // =========================================================
  // LIMPIAR FORMULARIO
  // =========================================================

  const handleReset = () => {

    setFormData({
      ...EMPTY_FORM,
      sucursales: [...EMPTY_FORM.sucursales],
    });

    setErrors({});
    setShowConfirmation(false);
    setShowDemoSuccess(false);
  };


  // =========================================================
  // VALIDAR FORMULARIO
  // =========================================================

  const handleSubmit = (event) => {

    event.preventDefault();

    setShowDemoSuccess(false);

    const validationErrors = validateForm(formData);

    setErrors(validationErrors);

    if (Object.keys(validationErrors).length > 0) {
      setShowConfirmation(false);
      return;
    }

    // Solo mostramos la confirmación.
    // No enviamos datos al backend.
    setShowConfirmation(true);
  };


  // =========================================================
  // CONFIRMACIÓN DE DEMOSTRACIÓN
  // =========================================================

  const handleDemoConfirm = () => {

    // Volvemos a validar por seguridad visual.
    const validationErrors = validateForm(formData);

    setErrors(validationErrors);

    if (
      Object.keys(validationErrors).length > 0 ||
      (!isSuperAdmin && formData.role !== "member")
    ) {
      setShowConfirmation(false);
      return;
    }

    setShowConfirmation(false);
    setShowDemoSuccess(true);
  };


  // =========================================================
  // INTERFAZ
  // =========================================================

  return (

    <div className="register-user-layout">

      {/* ================================================= */}
      {/* FORMULARIO */}
      {/* ================================================= */}

      <div className="register-user-form-card">

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>

          {/* ============================================= */}
          {/* INFORMACIÓN PERSONAL */}
          {/* ============================================= */}

          <section className="register-user-form__section">

            <div className="register-user-form__section-header">

              <span className="register-user-form__section-icon">
                <UserRound size={19} />
              </span>

              <div>
                <h2>Información personal</h2>
                <p>
                  Datos básicos de identificación del usuario.
                </p>
              </div>

            </div>


            <div className="register-user-form__grid">

              <div className="register-user-form__field">

                <label htmlFor="register-first-name">
                  Nombres <span>*</span>
                </label>

                <input
                  id="register-first-name"
                  type="text"
                  name="firstName"
                  value={formData.firstName}
                  onChange={handleChange}
                  placeholder="Ej. María Fernanda"
                  autoComplete="given-name"
                  maxLength={60}
                  aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.firstName)}
                />

                {errors.firstName && (
                  <small className="register-user-form__error">
                    {errors.firstName}
                  </small>
                )}

              </div>


              <div className="register-user-form__field">

                <label htmlFor="register-last-name">
                  Apellidos <span>*</span>
                </label>

                <input
                  id="register-last-name"
                  type="text"
                  name="lastName"
                  value={formData.lastName}
                  onChange={handleChange}
                  placeholder="Ej. González Pérez"
                  autoComplete="family-name"
                  maxLength={60}
                  aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.lastName)}
                />

                {errors.lastName && (
                  <small className="register-user-form__error">
                    {errors.lastName}
                  </small>
                )}

              </div>


              <div className="register-user-form__field">

                <label htmlFor="register-birth-date">
                  <CalendarDays size={14} />
                  Fecha de nacimiento <span>*</span>
                </label>

                <input
                  id="register-birth-date"
                  type="date"
                  name="birthDate"
                  value={formData.birthDate}
                  onChange={handleChange}
                  max={new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}
                  aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.birthDate)}
                />

                {errors.birthDate && (
                  <small className="register-user-form__error">
                    {errors.birthDate}
                  </small>
                )}

              </div>

            </div>

          </section>


          {/* ============================================= */}
          {/* CONTACTO */}
          {/* ============================================= */}

          <section className="register-user-form__section">

            <div className="register-user-form__section-header">

              <span className="register-user-form__section-icon">
                <Mail size={19} />
              </span>

              <div>
                <h2>Información de contacto</h2>
                <p>
                  Datos que permitirán contactar al usuario.
                </p>
              </div>

            </div>


            <div className="register-user-form__grid">

              <div className="register-user-form__field">

                <label htmlFor="register-email">
                  <Mail size={14} />
                  Correo electrónico <span>*</span>
                </label>

                <input
                  id="register-email"
                  type="email"
                  name="email"
                  value={formData.email}
                  onChange={handleChange}
                  placeholder="usuario@correo.cl"
                  autoComplete="email"
                  maxLength={150}
                  aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.email)}
                />

                {errors.email && (
                  <small className="register-user-form__error">
                    {errors.email}
                  </small>
                )}

              </div>


              <div className="register-user-form__field">

                <label htmlFor="register-phone">
                  <Phone size={14} />
                  Teléfono <span>*</span>
                </label>

                <input
                  id="register-phone"
                  type="tel"
                  name="phone"
                  value={formData.phone}
                  onChange={handleChange}
                  placeholder="+56 9 1234 5678"
                  autoComplete="tel"
                  maxLength={30}
                  aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.phone)}
                />

                {errors.phone && (
                  <small className="register-user-form__error">
                    {errors.phone}
                  </small>
                )}

              </div>

            </div>

          </section>


          {/* ============================================= */}
          {/* CONFIGURACIÓN DE CUENTA */}
          {/* ============================================= */}

          <section className="register-user-form__section">

            <div className="register-user-form__section-header">

              <span className="register-user-form__section-icon">
                <ShieldCheck size={19} />
              </span>

              <div>
                <h2>Configuración de cuenta</h2>
                <p>
                  Define el nivel de acceso y la sucursal.
                </p>
              </div>

            </div>


            <div className="register-user-form__field">

              <label htmlFor="register-role">
                Rol del usuario <span>*</span>
              </label>

              <select
                id="register-role"
                name="role"
                value={formData.role}
                onChange={handleChange}
                aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.role)}
              >

                {availableRoles.map((role) => (
                  <option
                    key={role.value}
                    value={role.value}
                  >
                    {role.label}
                  </option>
                ))}

              </select>

              <p className="register-user-form__hint">
                {formData.role === "admin"
                  ? "El administrador podrá acceder a las funciones administrativas autorizadas."
                  : "El miembro tendrá acceso a su espacio personal dentro de la comunidad."}
              </p>

              {errors.role && (
                <small className="register-user-form__error">
                  {errors.role}
                </small>
              )}

            </div>


            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* SUCURSALES */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            <div className="register-user-form__branches">

              <div className="register-user-form__subheading">
                <Building2 size={17} />
                <strong>Sucursales asignadas</strong>
              </div>

              <p className="register-user-form__hint">
                Selecciona las sucursales a las que
                pertenecerá el usuario.
              </p>


              <div className="register-user-form__branch-list">

                {branches.map((branch) => {

                  const branchId = String(
                    branch.id || branch._id
                  );

                  const checked =
                    formData.sucursales.includes(branchId);

                  return (

                    <label
                      className="register-user-form__branch"
                      key={branchId}
                    >

                      <input
                        type="checkbox"
                        checked={checked}
                        onChange={(event) =>
                          handleBranchChange(
                            branchId,
                            event.target.checked
                          )
                        }
                      />

                      <span className="register-user-form__branch-info">
                        <strong>{branch.name}</strong>
                        <small>{branch.code}</small>
                      </span>

                      {checked && (
                        <CheckCircle2 size={18} />
                      )}

                    </label>

                  );

                })}

              </div>

              {errors.sucursales && (
                <small className="register-user-form__error">
                  {errors.sucursales}
                </small>
              )}

            </div>


            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* SUCURSAL PRINCIPAL */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            <div className="register-user-form__field">

              <label htmlFor="register-main-branch">
                Sucursal principal <span>*</span>
              </label>

              <select
                id="register-main-branch"
                name="sucursalPrincipal"
                value={formData.sucursalPrincipal}
                onChange={handleChange}
                aria-invalid={
                  Boolean(errors.sucursalPrincipal)
                }
              >

                <option value="">
                  Seleccionar sucursal
                </option>

                {branches
                  .filter((branch) =>
                    formData.sucursales.includes(
                      String(branch.id || branch._id)
                    )
                  )
                  .map((branch) => (

                    <option
                      key={branch.id || branch._id}
                      value={String(branch.id || branch._id)}
                    >
                      {branch.name}
                    </option>

                  ))}

              </select>

              {errors.sucursalPrincipal && (
                <small className="register-user-form__error">
                  {errors.sucursalPrincipal}
                </small>
              )}

            </div>

          </section>


          {/* ============================================= */}
          {/* AVISO DE SEGURIDAD */}
          {/* ============================================= */}

          <div className="register-user-form__notice">

            <Info size={19} />

            <div>
              <strong>Activación de la cuenta</strong>
              <p>
                En la versión definitiva, el sistema
                gestionará el acceso inicial mediante
                un proceso seguro. Esta demostración
                no genera contraseñas ni invitaciones.
              </p>
            </div>

          </div>


          {/* ============================================= */}
          {/* ERRORES */}
          {/* ============================================= */}

          {hasErrors && (

            <div
              className="register-user-form__validation"
              role="alert"
            >
              <AlertCircle size={17} />
              Revisa los campos señalados antes de continuar.
            </div>

          )}


          {/* ============================================= */}
          {/* CONFIRMACIÓN */}
          {/* ============================================= */}

          {showConfirmation && (

            <div className="register-user-form__confirmation">

              <div className="register-user-form__confirmation-title">
                <ClipboardCheck size={20} />
                <strong>Confirmar registro de demostración</strong>
              </div>

              <p>
                Se ha validado la información de
                <strong> {fullName}</strong>.
                ¿Deseas visualizar el resultado
                de la simulación?
              </p>

              <div className="register-user-form__confirmation-actions">

                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="register-user-form__button-secondary"
                  onClick={() => setShowConfirmation(false)}
                >
                  Volver
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="register-user-form__button-primary"
                  onClick={handleDemoConfirm}
                >
                  <CheckCircle2 size={17} />
                  Confirmar demostración
                </button>

              </div>

            </div>

          )}


          {/* ============================================= */}
          {/* RESULTADO DE DEMOSTRACIÓN */}
          {/* ============================================= */}

          {showDemoSuccess && (

            <div
              className="register-user-form__demo-success"
              role="status"
            >

              <CheckCircle2 size={21} />

              <div>
                <strong>Formulario validado correctamente</strong>
                <p>
                  Demostración completada. No se creó
                  ninguna cuenta ni se almacenaron datos.
                </p>
              </div>

            </div>

          )}


          {/* ============================================= */}
          {/* ACCIONES */}
          {/* ============================================= */}

          <div className="register-user-form__actions">

            <button
              type="button"
              className="register-user-form__button-secondary"
              onClick={handleReset}
            >
              <RotateCcw size={17} />
              Limpiar formulario
            </button>

            <button
              type="submit"
              className="register-user-form__button-primary"
            >
              <UserPlus size={17} />
              Revisar registro
            </button>

          </div>

        </form>

      </div>


      {/* ================================================= */}
      {/* PANEL LATERAL DE VISTA PREVIA */}
      {/* ================================================= */}

      <aside className="register-user-preview">

        <div className="register-user-preview__heading">
          <ClipboardCheck size={18} />
          <h2>Vista previa de cuenta</h2>
        </div>

        <p className="register-user-preview__description">
          La información se actualiza mientras
          completas el formulario.
        </p>


        <div className="register-user-preview__profile">

          <div className="register-user-preview__avatar">
            {initials}
          </div>

          <strong>
            {fullName || "Nuevo usuario"}
          </strong>

          <span>
            {formData.email || "Correo sin ingresar"}
          </span>

          <div className="register-user-preview__role">
            <ShieldCheck size={14} />
            {getRoleLabel(formData.role)}
          </div>

        </div>


        <div className="register-user-preview__details">

          <div>
            <span>Estado inicial previsto</span>
            <strong>Activo</strong>
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Rol asignado</span>
            <strong>{getRoleLabel(formData.role)}</strong>
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Sucursal principal</span>
            <strong>
              {mainBranch?.name || "Sin seleccionar"}
            </strong>
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Sucursales seleccionadas</span>
            <strong>{formData.sucursales.length}</strong>
          </div>

        </div>


        <div className="register-user-preview__footer">
          <Info size={16} />
          <span>
            Vista previa únicamente. Ninguna cuenta
            será creada durante esta etapa.
          </span>
        </div>

      </aside>

    </div>

  );
}

export default RegisterUserForm;
