import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";

import {
  X,
  ShieldCheck,
  Mail,
  Phone,
  CalendarDays,
  Building2,
  Clock3,
  LockKeyhole,
  Info,
} from "lucide-react";

import "../../../styles/components/super-admin/administrators/AdministratorDetailsModal.css";


// =========================================================
// FUNCIONES AUXILIARES
// =========================================================

const formatDate = (value) => {

  if (!value) return "No registrado";

  const date = new Date(value);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    return "No registrado";
  }

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-CL", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "long",
    year: "numeric",
    timeZone: "UTC",
  }).format(date);
};


function DetailItem({ icon: Icon, label, value }) {

  return (
    <div className="administrator-details__item">
      <Icon size={17} aria-hidden="true" />

      <div>
        <span>{label}</span>
        <strong>{value || "No registrado"}</strong>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


// =========================================================
// MODAL
// =========================================================

function AdministratorDetailsModal({
  administrator,
  onClose,
}) {

  const dialogRef = useRef(null);
  const closeRef = useRef(null);

  const isSuperAdmin =
    administrator?.role === "super_admin";


  // =========================================================
  // ACCESIBILIDAD
  // =========================================================

  useEffect(() => {

    if (!administrator) return;

    const previousFocus = document.activeElement;
    const previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;

    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";

    closeRef.current?.focus();

    const handleKeyDown = (event) => {

      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        onClose();
      }

      if (event.key === "Tab") {

        const elements = Array.from(
          dialogRef.current?.querySelectorAll(
            'button:not([disabled]), [href], input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'
          ) || []
        );

        if (!elements.length) return;

        const first = elements[0];
        const last = elements[elements.length - 1];

        if (
          event.shiftKey &&
          document.activeElement === first
        ) {
          event.preventDefault();
          last.focus();
        } else if (
          !event.shiftKey &&
          document.activeElement === last
        ) {
          event.preventDefault();
          first.focus();
        }
      }

    };

    document.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);

    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow;
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
      previousFocus?.focus?.();
    };

  }, [administrator, onClose]);


  if (!administrator) return null;


  const fullName =
    `${administrator.firstName} ${administrator.lastName}`;

  const sucursales =
    Array.isArray(administrator.sucursales)
      ? administrator.sucursales
      : [];


  return (
    <div
      className="administrator-details__overlay"
      onMouseDown={(event) => {
        if (event.target === event.currentTarget) {
          onClose();
        }
      }}
    >

      <div
        className="administrator-details"
        ref={dialogRef}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="administrator-details-title"
        tabIndex={-1}
      >

        <header className="administrator-details__header">

          <div>
            <span className="administrator-details__eyebrow">
              Gestión administrativa
            </span>

            <h2 id="administrator-details-title">
              Detalles del administrador
            </h2>

            <p>
              Información de la cuenta y sus accesos.
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            ref={closeRef}
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Cerrar detalles"
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </button>

        </header>


        <div className="administrator-details__body">

          <div className="administrator-details__profile">

            <div className="administrator-details__avatar">
              {administrator.firstName?.[0]}
              {administrator.lastName?.[0]}
            </div>

            <div>
              <h3>{fullName}</h3>
              <p>{administrator.email}</p>

              <span className="administrator-details__role">
                <ShieldCheck size={14} />

                {isSuperAdmin
                  ? "Superadministrador"
                  : "Administrador"}
              </span>
            </div>

          </div>


          {isSuperAdmin && (
            <div className="administrator-details__protected">
              <LockKeyhole size={18} />
              <span>
                Cuenta protegida. Su modificación
                requiere controles especiales.
              </span>
            </div>
          )}


          <section className="administrator-details__section">

            <h3>Información personal</h3>

            <div className="administrator-details__grid">

              <DetailItem
                icon={Mail}
                label="Correo electrónico"
                value={administrator.email}
              />

              <DetailItem
                icon={Phone}
                label="Teléfono"
                value={administrator.phone}
              />

              <DetailItem
                icon={CalendarDays}
                label="Fecha de nacimiento"
                value={formatDate(administrator.birthDate)}
              />

              <DetailItem
                icon={Clock3}
                label="Último inicio de sesión"
                value={formatDate(administrator.lastLoginAt)}
              />

            </div>

          </section>


          <section className="administrator-details__section">

            <h3>Sucursales autorizadas</h3>

            <div className="administrator-details__branches">

              {sucursales.length > 0 ? (

                sucursales.map((branch) => {

                  const branchId =
                    branch._id || branch.id;

                  const mainBranchId =
                    administrator.sucursalPrincipal?._id ||
                    administrator.sucursalPrincipal?.id ||
                    administrator.sucursalPrincipal;

                  const isMain =
                    String(branchId) === String(mainBranchId);

                  return (
                    <div
                      key={branchId}
                      className="administrator-details__branch"
                    >
                      <Building2 size={17} />

                      <div>
                        <strong>{branch.name}</strong>
                        <span>{branch.code}</span>
                      </div>

                      {isMain && (
                        <span className="administrator-details__main">
                          Principal
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  );
                })

              ) : (

                <p className="administrator-details__empty">
                  No hay sucursales disponibles.
                </p>

              )}

            </div>

          </section>


          <section className="administrator-details__section">

            <h3>Estado de la cuenta</h3>

            <div className="administrator-details__grid">

              <DetailItem
                icon={ShieldCheck}
                label="Estado"
                value={
                  administrator.isActive
                    ? "Activa"
                    : "Desactivada"
                }
              />

              <DetailItem
                icon={CalendarDays}
                label="Fecha de registro"
                value={formatDate(administrator.createdAt)}
              />

            </div>

          </section>


          <div className="administrator-details__notice">
            <Info size={17} />
            <span>
              Esta vista es informativa. Las acciones
              administrativas estarán disponibles
              cuando implementemos sus controles.
            </span>
          </div>

        </div>


        <footer className="administrator-details__footer">

          <button type="button" onClick={onClose}>
            Cerrar
          </button>

        </footer>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

export default AdministratorDetailsModal;

} from "lucide-react";

import "../../../styles/components/super-admin/administrators/AdministratorsFilters.css";

function AdministratorsFilters({
  search,
  role,
  status,
  onSearchChange,
  onRoleChange,
  onStatusChange,
  onClear,
}) {

  const hasFilters =
    search.trim() !== "" ||
    role !== "all" ||
    status !== "all";

  return (
    <div className="administrators-filters">

      <div className="administrators-filters__search">
        <Search size={17} aria-hidden="true" />

        <input
          type="search"
          value={search}
          onChange={(event) =>
            onSearchChange(event.target.value)
          }
          placeholder="Buscar por nombre, correo o sucursal..."
          aria-label="Buscar administradores"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="administrators-filters__select">
        <Filter size={16} aria-hidden="true" />

        <select
          value={role}
          onChange={(event) =>
            onRoleChange(event.target.value)
          }
          aria-label="Filtrar por rol"
        >
          <option value="all">Todos los roles</option>
          <option value="admin">Administradores</option>
          <option value="super_admin">Superadministradores</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <div className="administrators-filters__select">
        <select
          value={status}
          onChange={(event) =>
            onStatusChange(event.target.value)
          }
          aria-label="Filtrar por estado"
        >
          <option value="all">Todos los estados</option>
          <option value="active">Activos</option>
          <option value="inactive">Desactivados</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <button
        type="button"
        className="administrators-filters__clear"
        onClick={onClear}
        disabled={!hasFilters}
      >
        <RotateCcw size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
        Limpiar
      </button>

    </div>
  );
}

export default AdministratorsFilters;

import {
  ShieldCheck,
  Shield,
  UserCheck,
  UserX,
} from "lucide-react";

import "../../../styles/components/super-admin/administrators/AdministratorsSummary.css";

function AdministratorsSummary({ administrators = [] }) {

  const stats = [
    {
      label: "Cuentas administrativas",
      value: administrators.length,
      icon: Shield,
      type: "total",
      description: "Administradores y superadministradores",
    },
    {
      label: "Administradores",
      value: administrators.filter(
        (user) => user.role === "admin"
      ).length,
      icon: ShieldCheck,
      type: "admins",
      description: "Cuentas con rol administrativo",
    },
    {
      label: "Cuentas activas",
      value: administrators.filter(
        (user) => user.isActive === true
      ).length,
      icon: UserCheck,
      type: "active",
      description: "Con acceso habilitado",
    },
    {
      label: "Cuentas desactivadas",
      value: administrators.filter(
        (user) => user.isActive === false
      ).length,
      icon: UserX,
      type: "inactive",
      description: "Sin acceso al sistema",
    },
  ];

  return (
    <section
      className="administrators-summary"
      aria-label="Resumen de cuentas administrativas"
    >
      {stats.map((stat) => {

        const Icon = stat.icon;

        return (
          <article
            key={stat.type}
            className={`administrators-summary__card administrators-summary__card--${stat.type}`}
          >
            <div className="administrators-summary__top">
              <span>{stat.label}</span>
              <div className="administrators-summary__icon">
                <Icon size={19} strokeWidth={1.8} />
              </div>
            </div>

            <strong className="administrators-summary__value">
              {stat.value}
            </strong>

            <p>{stat.description}</p>
          </article>
        );
      })}
    </section>
  );
}

export default AdministratorsSummary;




File: C:\Users\Liss\Desktop\iglesia-el-ancla\frontend\src\components\super-admin\administrators\AdministratorsTable.jsx


import { useState } from "react";

import {
  Eye,
  LockKeyhole,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  SearchX,
} from "lucide-react";

import "../../../styles/components/super-admin/administrators/AdministratorsTable.css";

const PAGE_SIZE = 6;


// =========================================================
// FUNCIONES AUXILIARES
// =========================================================

const formatDate = (value) => {

  if (!value) return "Sin registro";

  const date = new Date(value);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    return "Sin registro";
  }

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-CL", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "short",
    year: "numeric",
    timeZone: "UTC",
  }).format(date);
};


const getInitials = (user) => {

  return [user.firstName, user.lastName]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .slice(0, 2)
    .map((part) => part.charAt(0).toUpperCase())
    .join("");
};


// =========================================================
// TABLA
// =========================================================

function AdministratorsTable({
  administrators = [],
  onView,
}) {

  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);

  const totalPages = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.ceil(administrators.length / PAGE_SIZE)
  );

  // Si cambian los filtros, ajustamos la página
  // visible sin modificar el estado durante el render.
  const currentPage = Math.min(page, totalPages);

  const visibleAdministrators = administrators.slice(
    (currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE,
    currentPage * PAGE_SIZE
  );

  return (
    <div className="administrators-table">

      <div className="administrators-table__scroll">

        <table>

          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Administrador</th>
              <th>Rol</th>
              <th>Sucursal principal</th>
              <th>Estado</th>
              <th>Registro</th>
              <th>Acciones</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody>

            {visibleAdministrators.map((user) => {

              const isSuperAdmin =
                user.role === "super_admin";

              const mainBranch =
                user.sucursalPrincipal?.name ||
                "Sin asignar";

              return (
                <tr key={user._id || user.id}>

                  <td>
                    <div className="administrators-table__identity">

                      <div
                        className={`administrators-table__avatar ${
                          isSuperAdmin
                            ? "administrators-table__avatar--super"
                            : ""
                        }`}
                      >
                        {getInitials(user)}
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <strong>
                          {user.firstName} {user.lastName}
                        </strong>

                        <span>{user.email}</span>
                      </div>

                    </div>
                  </td>

                  <td>
                    <span
                      className={`administrators-table__role ${
                        isSuperAdmin
                          ? "administrators-table__role--super"
                          : ""
                      }`}
                    >
                      {isSuperAdmin && (
                        <LockKeyhole size={12} />
                      )}

                      {isSuperAdmin
                        ? "Superadministrador"
                        : "Administrador"}
                    </span>
                  </td>

                  <td>
                    <span className="administrators-table__branch">
                      {mainBranch}
                    </span>
                  </td>

                  <td>
                    <span
                      className={`administrators-table__status ${
                        user.isActive
                          ? "administrators-table__status--active"
                          : "administrators-table__status--inactive"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {user.isActive
                        ? "Activo"
                        : "Desactivado"}
                    </span>
                  </td>

                  <td>
                    <span className="administrators-table__date">
                      {formatDate(user.createdAt)}
                    </span>
                  </td>

                  <td>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="administrators-table__view"
                      onClick={() => onView?.(user)}
                      aria-label={`Ver detalles de ${user.firstName} ${user.lastName}`}
                      title="Ver detalles"
                    >
                      <Eye size={17} />
                    </button>
                  </td>

                </tr>
              );
            })}

          </tbody>

        </table>

      </div>


      {/* ================================================= */}
      {/* ESTADO VACÍO */}
      {/* ================================================= */}

      {administrators.length === 0 && (

        <div className="administrators-table__empty">
          <SearchX size={29} />
          <strong>No se encontraron administradores</strong>
          <p>
            Prueba modificando los filtros de búsqueda.
          </p>
        </div>

      )}


      {/* ================================================= */}
      {/* PAGINACIÓN */}
      {/* ================================================= */}

      {administrators.length > 0 && (

        <footer className="administrators-table__footer">

          <span>
            Mostrando{" "}
            {((currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE) + 1}–
            {Math.min(
              currentPage * PAGE_SIZE,
              administrators.length
            )}{" "}
            de {administrators.length} cuentas
          </span>

          <div className="administrators-table__pagination">

            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={currentPage === 1}
              onClick={() =>
                setPage(currentPage - 1)
              }
              aria-label="Página anterior"
            >
              <ChevronLeft size={17} />
            </button>

            <span>
              {currentPage} / {totalPages}
            </span>

            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={currentPage === totalPages}
              onClick={() =>
                setPage(currentPage + 1)
              }
              aria-label="Página siguiente"
            >
              <ChevronRight size={17} />
            </button>

          </div>

        </footer>

      )}

    </div>
  );
}

export default AdministratorsTable;

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";

import {
  Check,
  Search,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";

import {
  PERMISSION_CATEGORIES,
  SYSTEM_PERMISSIONS,
  SYSTEM_ROLES,
} from "../../../data/admin/rolesPermissions";

import "../../../styles/components/super-admin/roles/PermissionsMatrix.css";

function PermissionsMatrix({ selectedRole }) {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("Todas");

  // =========================================================
  // FILTRADO DE PERMISOS
  // =========================================================

  const filteredPermissions = useMemo(() => {
    const normalizedSearch =
      search.trim().toLocaleLowerCase("es");

    return SYSTEM_PERMISSIONS.filter((permission) => {
      const matchesCategory =
        category === "Todas" ||
        permission.category === category;

      const searchableText = [
        permission.name,
        permission.description,
        permission.category,
      ]
        .join(" ")
        .toLocaleLowerCase("es");

      const matchesSearch =
        searchableText.includes(normalizedSearch);

      return matchesCategory && matchesSearch;
    });
  }, [search, category]);

  // =========================================================
  // AGRUPACIÓN POR CATEGORÍA
  // =========================================================

  const groupedPermissions = useMemo(() => {
    return filteredPermissions.reduce(
      (groups, permission) => {
        if (!groups[permission.category]) {
          groups[permission.category] = [];
        }

        groups[permission.category].push(permission);

        return groups;
      },
      {}
    );
  }, [filteredPermissions]);

  return (
    <section className="permissions-matrix">
      <div className="permissions-matrix__heading">
        <div>
          <h2>Matriz de permisos</h2>

          <p>
            Consulta los accesos propuestos para
            cada rol y módulo del sistema.
          </p>
        </div>

        <span className="permissions-matrix__counter">
          {filteredPermissions.length} permisos
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* ================================================= */}
      {/* FILTROS */}
      {/* ================================================= */}

      <div className="permissions-matrix__filters">
        <label className="permissions-matrix__search">
          <Search
            size={18}
            aria-hidden="true"
          />

          <input
            type="search"
            value={search}
            onChange={(event) =>
              setSearch(event.target.value)
            }
            placeholder="Buscar un permiso..."
            aria-label="Buscar permisos"
          />
        </label>

        <select
          className="permissions-matrix__select"
          value={category}
          onChange={(event) =>
            setCategory(event.target.value)
          }
          aria-label="Filtrar por categoría"
        >
          {PERMISSION_CATEGORIES.map((item) => (
            <option key={item} value={item}>
              {item}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>

      {/* ================================================= */}
      {/* TABLA DE PERMISOS */}
      {/* ================================================= */}

      <div className="permissions-matrix__table-wrapper">
        <table className="permissions-matrix__table">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th scope="col">
                Módulo / Permiso
              </th>

              {SYSTEM_ROLES.map((role) => (
                <th
                  scope="col"
                  key={role.id}
                  className={
                    selectedRole === role.id
                      ? "permissions-matrix__selected"
                      : ""
                  }
                >
                  {role.name}
                </th>
              ))}
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody>
            {Object.entries(groupedPermissions).map(
              ([groupName, permissions]) => (
                <FragmentRows
                  key={groupName}
                  groupName={groupName}
                  permissions={permissions}
                  selectedRole={selectedRole}
                />
              )
            )}

            {filteredPermissions.length === 0 && (
              <tr>
                <td
                  colSpan={SYSTEM_ROLES.length + 1}
                  className="permissions-matrix__empty"
                >
                  No se encontraron permisos con
                  los filtros seleccionados.
                </td>
              </tr>
            )}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      <p className="permissions-matrix__note">
        Esta matriz es una propuesta funcional.
        No modifica ni representa una verificación
        de los permisos efectivos del servidor.
      </p>
    </section>
  );
}

// =========================================================
// FILAS AGRUPADAS POR CATEGORÍA
// =========================================================

function FragmentRows({
  groupName,
  permissions,
  selectedRole,
}) {
  return (
    <>
      <tr className="permissions-matrix__group">
        <th
          scope="rowgroup"
          colSpan={SYSTEM_ROLES.length + 1}
        >
          {groupName}
        </th>
      </tr>

      {permissions.map((permission) => (
        <tr key={permission.id}>
          <td>
            <strong>{permission.name}</strong>

            <span>{permission.description}</span>
          </td>

          {SYSTEM_ROLES.map((role) => {
            const isAllowed =
              permission.roles[role.id] === true;

            return (
              <td
                key={role.id}
                className={
                  selectedRole === role.id
                    ? "permissions-matrix__selected"
                    : ""
                }
              >
                <span
                  className={[
                    "permissions-matrix__status",
                    isAllowed
                      ? "permissions-matrix__status--allowed"
                      : "permissions-matrix__status--denied",
                  ].join(" ")}
                  aria-label={
                    isAllowed
                      ? "Permiso propuesto"
                      : "Permiso no contemplado"
                  }
                  title={
                    isAllowed
                      ? "Permiso propuesto"
                      : "Permiso no contemplado"
                  }
                >
                  {isAllowed ? (
                    <Check size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
                  ) : (
                    <X size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
                  )}
                </span>
              </td>
            );
          })}
        </tr>
      ))}
    </>
  );
}

export default PermissionsMatrix;

import {
  Crown,
  Shield,
  UserRound,
} from "lucide-react";

import {
  SYSTEM_ROLES,
} from "../../../data/admin/rolesPermissions";

import "../../../styles/components/super-admin/roles/RolesCards.css";

const ROLE_ICONS = {
  member: UserRound,
  admin: Shield,
  super_admin: Crown,
};

function RolesCards({
  selectedRole,
  onSelectRole,
}) {
  return (
    <div className="roles-cards">
      {SYSTEM_ROLES.map((role) => {
        const Icon = ROLE_ICONS[role.id];

        const isSelected =
          selectedRole === role.id;

        return (
          <button
            key={role.id}
            type="button"
            aria-pressed={isSelected}
            className={[
              "roles-card",
              isSelected
                ? "roles-card--selected"
                : "",
            ].join(" ")}
            onClick={() => onSelectRole(role.id)}
          >
            <div className="roles-card__top">
              <div className="roles-card__icon">
                <Icon
                  size={23}
                  strokeWidth={1.8}
                  aria-hidden="true"
                />
              </div>

              <span className="roles-card__level">
                Nivel {role.level}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="roles-card__content">
              <h3>{role.name}</h3>

              <p>{role.description}</p>
            </div>

            <div className="roles-card__footer">
              <span>{role.scope}</span>

              {role.protected && (
                <strong>Protegido</strong>
              )}
            </div>
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

export default RolesCards;




File: C:\Users\Liss\Desktop\iglesia-el-ancla\frontend\src\components\super-admin\roles\RolesSummary.jsx


import {
  KeyRound,
  ShieldCheck,
  UsersRound,
} from "lucide-react";

import {
  SYSTEM_PERMISSIONS,
  SYSTEM_ROLES,
} from "../../../data/admin/rolesPermissions";

import "../../../styles/components/super-admin/roles/RolesSummary.css";

function RolesSummary() {
  const protectedRoles = SYSTEM_ROLES.filter(
    (role) => role.protected
  ).length;

  const summary = [
    {
      id: "roles",
      label: "Roles del sistema",
      value: SYSTEM_ROLES.length,
      icon: UsersRound,
      description: "Niveles de acceso definidos",
    },
    {
      id: "permissions",
      label: "Permisos documentados",
      value: SYSTEM_PERMISSIONS.length,
      icon: KeyRound,
      description: "Operaciones en la matriz propuesta",
    },
    {
      id: "protected",
      label: "Roles privilegiados",
      value: protectedRoles,
      icon: ShieldCheck,
      description: "Roles con protección especial",
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="roles-summary">
      {summary.map((item) => {
        const Icon = item.icon;

        return (
          <article
            className="roles-summary__card"
            key={item.id}
          >
            <div className="roles-summary__icon">
              <Icon
                size={22}
                strokeWidth={1.8}
                aria-hidden="true"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="roles-summary__information">
              <span className="roles-summary__label">
                {item.label}
              </span>

              <strong className="roles-summary__value">
                {item.value}
              </strong>

              <small>
                {item.description}
              </small>
            </div>
          </article>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

export default RolesSummary;

import {
  Building2,
  Church,
  LockKeyhole,
  SlidersHorizontal,
} from "lucide-react";

import "../../../styles/components/super-admin/settings/SettingsNavigation.css";

// =========================================================
// SECCIONES DISPONIBLES
// =========================================================

export const SETTINGS_SECTIONS = [
  {
    id: "general",
    title: "Información general",
    description: "Identidad y datos institucionales",
    icon: Church,
  },
  {
    id: "branches",
    title: "Sucursales",
    description: "Sedes y organización territorial",
    icon: Building2,
  },
  {
    id: "security",
    title: "Seguridad",
    description: "Sesiones y protección de cuentas",
    icon: LockKeyhole,
  },
  {
    id: "preferences",
    title: "Preferencias",
    description: "Parámetros de funcionamiento",
    icon: SlidersHorizontal,
  },
];

// =========================================================
// COMPONENTE PRINCIPAL
// =========================================================

function SettingsNavigation({
  activeSection,
  onSectionChange,
}) {
  return (
    <nav
      className="settings-navigation"
      aria-label="Secciones de configuración"
    >
      <div className="settings-navigation__heading">
        <span>CONFIGURACIÓN</span>
        <h2>Secciones</h2>
      </div>

      <div className="settings-navigation__items">
        {SETTINGS_SECTIONS.map((section) => {
          const Icon = section.icon;

          const isActive =
            activeSection === section.id;

          return (
            <button
              key={section.id}
              type="button"
              className={[
                "settings-navigation__item",
                isActive
                  ? "settings-navigation__item--active"
                  : "",
              ].join(" ")}
              onClick={() =>
                onSectionChange(section.id)
              }
              aria-current={
                isActive ? "page" : undefined
              }
            >
              <span className="settings-navigation__icon">
                <Icon
                  size={20}
                  strokeWidth={1.8}
                  aria-hidden="true"
                />
              </span>

              <span className="settings-navigation__content">
                <strong>{section.title}</strong>
                <small>{section.description}</small>
              </span>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}

export default SettingsNavigation;

function InformationField({ label, value, note }) {
  return (
    <div className="settings-section__field">
      <span>{label}</span>

      <strong>{value || "Por configurar"}</strong>

      {note && <small>{note}</small>}
    </div>
  );
}

// =========================================================
// COMPONENTE AUXILIAR: TARJETA DE CONFIGURACIÓN
// =========================================================

function SettingCard({
  icon: Icon,
  title,
  description,
  status = "Pendiente de integración",
}) {
  return (
    <article className="settings-section__option">
      <div className="settings-section__option-icon">
        <Icon
          size={21}
          strokeWidth={1.8}
          aria-hidden="true"
        />

      <div className="settings-section__option-content">
        <h3>{title}</h3>
        <p>{description}</p>
        <span>{status}</span>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

  general: GeneralSettings,
  branches: BranchSettings,
  security: SecuritySettings,
  preferences: PreferencesSettings,
};

    SECTION_COMPONENTS[activeSection] ||
    GeneralSettings;

  return (

      <div className="settings-section__footer">
        <Info size={17} aria-hidden="true" />

        <span>
          Vista de referencia. No se realizan
          modificaciones en la base de datos.
        </span>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

// =====================================================
// CATEGORÍAS DISPONIBLES
// =====================================================

export const downloadableMaterialCategories = [
    "Todos",
    ...new Set(
        downloadableMaterials.map(
            (material) => material.category
        )
    ),
];

// =====================================================
// BÚSQUEDA POR SLUG
// =====================================================

export const getDownloadableMaterialBySlug = (slug) =>
    downloadableMaterials.find(
        (material) => material.slug === slug
    );

// =====================================================
// MATERIALES RELACIONADOS
// =====================================================

export const getRelatedDownloadableMaterials = (
    relatedSlugs = []
) =>
    downloadableMaterials.filter((material) =>
        relatedSlugs.includes(material.slug)
    );

// =====================================================
// EXPORTACIÓN PRINCIPAL
// =====================================================

export default downloadableMaterials;

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";

import {
  Link,
  useLocation,
} from "react-router-dom";

import {
  ArrowLeft,
  History,
  Info,
  LockKeyhole,
  ShieldCheck,
} from "lucide-react";

// =========================================================
// COMPONENTES
// =========================================================

import ActivityFilters from "../../components/administration/activity/ActivityFilters";

import ActivityTable from "../../components/administration/activity/ActivityTable";

// =========================================================
// ESTILOS
// =========================================================

import "../../styles/pages/administration/ActivityHistory.css";

// =========================================================
// DATOS TEMPORALES DE DEMOSTRACIÓN
// =========================================================

// Estos registros son completamente ficticios.
// Se reemplazarán por datos obtenidos del backend.
//
// createdAt representa la fecha y hora exactas
// en que se completó una modificación.

const DEMO_ACTIVITIES = [
  {
    id: "demo-001",
    actorName: "María González",
    actorEmail: "maria@example.com",
    createdAt: "2026-10-08T18:45:00Z",

    description:
      "Desactivó una cuenta de usuario.",
  },
];

// =========================================================
// UTILIDAD PARA FILTRAR POR FECHA EN CHILE
// =========================================================

const DATE_FILTER_FORMATTER = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(
  "en-CA",
  {
    timeZone: "America/Santiago",
    year: "numeric",
    month: "2-digit",
    day: "2-digit",
  }
);

function getChileDate(isoDate) {
  const parts = DATE_FILTER_FORMATTER.formatToParts(
    new Date(isoDate)
  );

  const values = Object.fromEntries(
    parts.map((part) => [part.type, part.value])
  );

  return `${values.year}-${values.month}-${values.day}`;
}

// =========================================================
// PÁGINA PRINCIPAL
// =========================================================

function ActivityHistory() {
  const location = useLocation();

  // Identificamos el panel desde el cual
  // se está visualizando el historial.

  const isSuperAdminPanel =
    location.pathname === "/super-admin" ||
    location.pathname.startsWith("/super-admin/");

  const panelPath = isSuperAdminPanel
    ? "/super-admin"
    : "/admin";

  // =========================================================
  // ESTADOS DE LOS FILTROS
  // =========================================================

  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState("");

  // =========================================================
  // FILTRADO DE REGISTROS
  // =========================================================

  const filteredActivities = useMemo(() => {
    const normalizedSearch =
      search.trim().toLocaleLowerCase("es-CL");

    return DEMO_ACTIVITIES
      .filter((activity) => {
        const searchableText = [
          activity.actorName,
          activity.actorEmail,
          activity.description,
        ]
          .join(" ")
          .toLocaleLowerCase("es-CL");

        const matchesSearch =
          !normalizedSearch ||
          searchableText.includes(normalizedSearch);

        const matchesDate =
          !selectedDate ||
          getChileDate(activity.createdAt) ===
            selectedDate;

        return matchesSearch && matchesDate;
      })
      .sort(
        (a, b) =>
          new Date(b.createdAt).getTime() -
          new Date(a.createdAt).getTime()
      );
  }, [search, selectedDate]);

  // =========================================================
  // LIMPIAR FILTROS
  // =========================================================

  const handleResetFilters = () => {
    setSearch("");
    setSelectedDate("");
  };

  // =========================================================
  // INTERFAZ
  // =========================================================

  return (
    <div className="activity-history-page">

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* VOLVER AL PANEL */}
      {/* ============================================= */}

      <div className="activity-history-page__navigation">
        <Link
          to={panelPath}
          className="activity-history-page__back"
        >
          <ArrowLeft
            size={17}
            strokeWidth={2}
            aria-hidden="true"
          />

          <span>Volver al panel</span>
        </Link>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* ENCABEZADO */}
      {/* ============================================= */}

      <header className="activity-history-page__header">
        <div>
          <span className="activity-history-page__eyebrow">
            ADMINISTRACIÓN / AUDITORÍA
          </span>

          <h1>
            <History
              size={29}
              strokeWidth={1.8}
              aria-hidden="true"
            />

            Historial de actividad
          </h1>

          <p>
            Consulta quién realizó modificaciones
            en el sistema, cuándo ocurrieron
            y qué información fue modificada.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="activity-history-page__badge">
          <ShieldCheck
            size={17}
            aria-hidden="true"
          />

          <span>Solo lectura</span>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* AVISO DE DEMOSTRACIÓN */}
      {/* ============================================= */}

      <div
        className="activity-history-page__notice"
        role="note"
      >
        <Info
          size={20}
          aria-hidden="true"
        />

        <div>
          <strong>
            Registros de demostración
          </strong>

          <p>
            Los registros mostrados son ficticios
            y permiten visualizar el diseño.
            El historial real se cargará desde
            el backend cuando implementemos
            la auditoría de modificaciones.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* FILTROS */}
      {/* ============================================= */}

      <ActivityFilters
        search={search}
        onSearchChange={setSearch}
        selectedDate={selectedDate}
        onDateChange={setSelectedDate}
        onReset={handleResetFilters}
      />

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* TABLA DE REGISTROS */}
      {/* ============================================= */}

      <ActivityTable
        activities={filteredActivities}
      />

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* INFORMACIÓN DE SEGURIDAD */}
      {/* ============================================= */}

      <footer className="activity-history-page__footer">
        <LockKeyhole
          size={18}
          aria-hidden="true"
        />

        <p>
          El historial es exclusivamente de consulta.
          Los registros reales deberán generarse
          y protegerse desde el servidor, sin
          permitir modificaciones desde este módulo.
        </p>
      </footer>

    </div>
  );
}

export default ActivityHistory;

// Importamos los iconos utilizados en el dashboard.

  CalendarDays,
  HandHeart,
  Users,

  ArrowRight,
  Clock3,
  MapPin,
  CircleDollarSign,
} from "lucide-react";

// Importamos los gastos temporales
// que actualmente simulan la información de MongoDB.
import {
  expenses,
} from "../../data/admin/expensesData";

// Importamos los estilos exclusivos de la página.
import "../../styles/pages/super-admin/AdminDashboard.css";


// Creamos el componente principal
// de la página de inicio administrativa.
function AdminDashboard() {

  // ---------------------------------------------------------
  // DATOS TEMPORALES DE EVENTOS
  // ---------------------------------------------------------

  // Estos eventos serán reemplazados posteriormente
  // por información obtenida desde MongoDB.
  const upcomingEvents = [
    {
      // Identificador temporal del evento.
      id: 1,

      // Nombre de la actividad.
      title: "Culto general",

      // Día en el que se realizará.
      date: "Domingo",

      // Hora de inicio.
      time: "11:00 hrs",

      // Modalidad o ubicación.
      location: "Presencial y online",
    },
    {
      id: 2,
      title: "Descubriendo la Biblia",
      date: "Martes",
      time: "20:00 hrs",
      location: "Online",
    },
    {
      id: 3,
      title: "Reunión de oración",
      date: "Miércoles",
      time: "21:00 hrs",
      location: "Online",
    },
    {
      id: 4,
      title: "Estudio bíblico",
      date: "Jueves",
      time: "20:00 hrs",
      location: "Online",
    },
  ];


  // ---------------------------------------------------------
  // PETICIONES DE ORACIÓN TEMPORALES
  // ---------------------------------------------------------

  // Estas peticiones también llegarán posteriormente
  // desde la API del backend.
  const recentPrayerRequests = [
    {
      // Identificador temporal.
      id: 1,

      // Nombre mostrado al administrador.
      requester: "Solicitud anónima",

      // Texto resumido de la petición.
      summary:
        "Oración por una situación familiar y dirección para tomar decisiones.",

      // Estado actual de la solicitud.

      // Tiempo transcurrido desde su recepción.
      receivedAt: "Hace 2 horas",
    },
    {
      id: 2,
      requester: "María",

        "Oración por la recuperación de un familiar que se encuentra enfermo.",

      status: "En oración",
      receivedAt: "Hace 5 horas",
    },
    {
      id: 3,
      requester: "Solicitud privada",
      summary:
        "Esta petición contiene información privada para el equipo de liderazgo.",

      status: "Pendiente",
      receivedAt: "Ayer",
    },
  ];


  // ---------------------------------------------------------
  // FORMATEADOR DE MONEDA
  // ---------------------------------------------------------

  // Creamos una función reutilizable para mostrar
  // valores utilizando pesos chilenos.
  const formatCurrency = (value) => {

    // Utilizamos Intl.NumberFormat para aplicar
    // correctamente el formato monetario nacional.
    return new Intl.NumberFormat("es-CL", {

      // Indicamos que queremos mostrar una moneda.
      style: "currency",

      // Definimos el peso chileno.
      currency: "CLP",

      // Evitamos mostrar decimales.
      maximumFractionDigits: 0,

    }).format(value);
  };


  // ---------------------------------------------------------
  // RESUMEN DE GASTOS
  // ---------------------------------------------------------

  // Calculamos los valores financieros utilizando
  // los gastos temporales que ya existen en el proyecto.
  const financialSummary = useMemo(() => {

    // Sumamos todos los gastos que fueron pagados.
    const paidTotal = expenses

      // Dejamos solamente los movimientos pagados.
      .filter(
        (expense) =>
          expense.status === "Pagado"
      )

      // Sumamos sus montos.
      .reduce(
        (total, expense) =>
          total + expense.amount,
        0
      );


    // Sumamos todos los gastos pendientes.
    const pendingTotal = expenses

      // Dejamos solamente los movimientos pendientes.
      .filter(
        (expense) =>
          expense.status === "Pendiente"
      )

      // Sumamos sus montos.
      .reduce(
        (total, expense) =>
          total + expense.amount,
        0
      );


    // Contamos cuántos gastos siguen pendientes.
    const pendingCount = expenses.filter(
      (expense) =>
        expense.status === "Pendiente"
    ).length;


    // Retornamos todos los resultados calculados.
    return {
      paidTotal,
      pendingTotal,
      pendingCount,
    };

              </button>
            );

          })}

        </div>

      </section>


      {/* ================================================= */}
      {/* CONTENIDO PRINCIPAL EN DOS COLUMNAS */}
      {/* ================================================= */}

      <div className="admin-dashboard__columns">

        {/* ================================================= */}
        {/* PRÓXIMAS ACTIVIDADES */}
        {/* ================================================= */}

        <section className="admin-dashboard-section">

          {/* Encabezado de la sección. */}
          <div className="admin-dashboard-section__header">

            {/* Agrupamos los textos. */}
            <div>

              {/* Título de la sección. */}
              <h2>
                Próximas actividades
              </h2>

              {/* Descripción complementaria. */}
              <p>
                Reuniones programadas para la comunidad.
              </p>

            </div>


            {/* Botón pendiente hasta crear
                la gestión administrativa de eventos. */}
            <button
              className="admin-dashboard-section__link"
              type="button"
              disabled
            >
              Ver todas
            </button>

          </div>


          {/* Creamos la lista de eventos. */}
          <div className="admin-dashboard-events">

            {/* Recorremos los eventos temporales. */}
            {upcomingEvents.map((event) => (

              // Creamos una fila por evento.
              <article
                className="admin-dashboard-event"
                key={event.id}
              >

                {/* Mostramos un icono de calendario. */}
                <div className="admin-dashboard-event__icon">
                  <CalendarDays
                    size={21}
                    strokeWidth={1.8}
                    aria-hidden="true"
                  />
                </div>


                {/* Mostramos la información del evento. */}
                <div className="admin-dashboard-event__content">

                  {/* Nombre de la actividad. */}
                  <h3>
                    {event.title}
                  </h3>


                  {/* Agrupamos fecha y hora. */}
                  <div className="admin-dashboard-event__details">

                    {/* Mostramos el día y la hora. */}
                    <span>
                      <Clock3
                        size={14}
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      />

                      {event.date}, {event.time}
                    </span>


                    {/* Mostramos la modalidad. */}
                    <span>
                      <MapPin
                        size={14}
                        aria-hidden="true"
                      />

                      {event.location}
                    </span>

                  </div>

                </div>

              </article>

            ))}

          </div>

        </section>


        {/* ================================================= */}
        {/* PETICIONES DE ORACIÓN */}
        {/* ================================================= */}

        <section className="admin-dashboard-section">

          {/* Encabezado de la sección. */}
          <div className="admin-dashboard-section__header">

            {/* Agrupamos título y descripción. */}
            <div>

              {/* Título principal. */}
              <h2>
                Peticiones recientes
              </h2>

              {/* Texto complementario. */}
              <p>
                Solicitudes recibidas desde la comunidad.
              </p>

            </div>


            {/* Esta acción se habilitará
                cuando construyamos el módulo correspondiente. */}
            <button
              className="admin-dashboard-section__link"
              type="button"
              disabled
            >
              Ver todas
            </button>

          </div>


          {/* Creamos la lista de peticiones. */}
          <div className="admin-dashboard-prayers">

            {/* Recorremos las peticiones temporales. */}
            {recentPrayerRequests.map((request) => (

              // Creamos una tarjeta por petición.
              <article
                className="admin-dashboard-prayer"
                key={request.id}
              >

                {/* Creamos el encabezado de la petición. */}
                <div className="admin-dashboard-prayer__header">

                  {/* Mostramos quién envió la solicitud. */}
                  <strong>
                    {request.requester}
                  </strong>

                  {/* Mostramos el estado actual. */}
                  <span
                    className={`
                      admin-dashboard-prayer__status
                      ${
                        request.status === "Pendiente"
                          ? "admin-dashboard-prayer__status--pending"
                          : "admin-dashboard-prayer__status--active"
                      }
                    `}
                  >
                    {request.status}
                  </span>

                </div>


                {/* Mostramos el resumen de la solicitud. */}
                <p>
                  {request.summary}
                </p>


                {/* Mostramos cuándo fue recibida. */}
                <span className="admin-dashboard-prayer__time">
                  {request.receivedAt}
                </span>

              </article>

            ))}

          </div>

        </section>

      </div>


      {/* ================================================= */}
      {/* RESUMEN FINANCIERO */}
      {/* ================================================= */}

      <section className="admin-dashboard-section">

        {/* Encabezado del resumen financiero. */}
        <div className="admin-dashboard-section__header">

          {/* Agrupamos título y descripción. */}
          <div>

            {/* Título principal. */}
            <h2>
              Resumen financiero
            </h2>

            {/* Explicamos el origen temporal de la información. */}
            <p>
              Vista general de los gastos registrados.
            </p>

          </div>


          {/* Permitimos ingresar al módulo existente. */}
          <Link
            className="admin-dashboard-section__link"
            to="/admin/finanzas/gastos"
          >
            Ver gastos

            <ArrowRight
              size={16}
              strokeWidth={2}
              aria-hidden="true"
            />
          </Link>

        </div>


        {/* Mostramos los indicadores financieros. */}
        <div className="admin-dashboard-financial">

          {/* Tarjeta de gastos pagados. */}
          <article className="admin-dashboard-financial__item">

            {/* Mostramos el icono. */}
            <div className="admin-dashboard-financial__icon">
              <CircleDollarSign
                size={23}
                strokeWidth={1.8}
                aria-hidden="true"
              />
            </div>


            {/* Mostramos la información. */}
            <div>
              <span>
                Gastos pagados
              </span>

              <strong>
                {formatCurrency(
                  financialSummary.paidTotal
                )}
              </strong>
            </div>

          </article>


          {/* Tarjeta de gastos pendientes. */}
          <article className="admin-dashboard-financial__item">

            {/* Mostramos el icono. */}
            <div className="admin-dashboard-financial__icon">
              <WalletCards
                size={23}
                strokeWidth={1.8}
                aria-hidden="true"
              />
            </div>


            {/* Mostramos la información. */}
            <div>
              <span>
                Total comprometido
              </span>

              <strong>
                {formatCurrency(
                  financialSummary.pendingTotal
                )}
              </strong>
            </div>

          </article>


          {/* Tarjeta con cantidad de movimientos. */}
          <article className="admin-dashboard-financial__item">

            {/* Mostramos el icono. */}
            <div className="admin-dashboard-financial__icon">
              <ReceiptText
                size={23}
                strokeWidth={1.8}
                aria-hidden="true"
              />
            </div>


            {/* Mostramos la información. */}
            <div>
              <span>
                Movimientos registrados
              </span>

              <strong>
                {expenses.length}
              </strong>
            </div>

          </article>

        </div>

      </section>

    </main>
  );
}


// Exportamos la página para utilizarla en App.js.
export default AdminDashboard;
