// =========================================================
// UTILIDADES DE FORMATO
// =========================================================

function formatEventDate(dateString) {
  if (!dateString) {
    return "Sin fecha";
  }

  // Usamos mediodía UTC para evitar que
  // una fecha YYYY-MM-DD cambie de día
  // al representarse en Chile.

  const date = new Date(`${dateString}T12:00:00Z`);

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-CL", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "short",
    year: "numeric",
    timeZone: "UTC",
  }).format(date);
}

// =========================================================
// COMPONENTE PRINCIPAL
// =========================================================

function EventsManagement() {
  const location = useLocation();
  // =========================================================
  // ESTADO DE EVENTOS
  // =========================================================

  // Iniciamos con los eventos de demostración.
  // Los cambios permanecerán en memoria mientras
  // la aplicación siga abierta.

  const [events, setEvents] = useState(DEMO_EVENTS);

  // =========================================================
  // ESTADO DEL FORMULARIO
  // =========================================================

  // Controla si el modal está abierto.
  const [isFormOpen, setIsFormOpen] = useState(false);

  // Almacena el evento seleccionado para edición.
  // null significa que estamos creando uno nuevo.
  const [selectedEvent, setSelectedEvent] = useState(null);

  // =========================================================
  // ESTADO DE LA VISTA DE DETALLES
  // =========================================================

  // Guarda el identificador del evento que queremos consultar.
  const [detailsEventId, setDetailsEventId] = useState(null);

  // Obtiene siempre la versión actualizada del evento.
  const detailsEvent =
    events.find((event) => event.id === detailsEventId) ?? null;

  // =========================================================
  // ESTADO DE CONFIRMACIONES
  // =========================================================

  const [confirmationsEventId, setConfirmationsEventId] =
    useState(null);

  const confirmationsEvent =
    events.find(
      (event) => event.id === confirmationsEventId
    ) ?? null;

  function handleViewConfirmations(event) {
    setConfirmationsEventId(event.id);
  }

  function handleCloseConfirmations() {
    setConfirmationsEventId(null);
  }

  // =========================================================
  // IDENTIFICACIÓN DEL PANEL
  // =========================================================

  const isSuperAdminPanel =
    location.pathname === "/super-admin" ||
    location.pathname.startsWith("/super-admin/");

  const adminBasePath = isSuperAdminPanel
    ? "/super-admin"
    : "/admin";

  // =========================================================
  // ESTADO DEL BUSCADOR
  // =========================================================

  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");

  // =========================================================
  // FILTROS AVANZADOS
  // =========================================================

  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("all");
  const [frequencyFilter, setFrequencyFilter] = useState("all");
  const [modalityFilter, setModalityFilter] = useState("all");

  // Restablecer todos los filtros.
  function handleClearFilters() {
    setSearch("");
    setStatusFilter("all");
    setFrequencyFilter("all");
    setModalityFilter("all");
  }

  // =========================================================
  // ABRIR FORMULARIO PARA CREAR
  // =========================================================

  function handleCreateEvent() {
    setSelectedEvent(null);
    setIsFormOpen(true);
  }

  // =========================================================
  // ABRIR FORMULARIO PARA EDITAR
  // =========================================================

  function handleEditEvent(event) {
    setSelectedEvent(event);
    setIsFormOpen(true);
  }

  // =========================================================
  // ABRIR DETALLES DEL EVENTO
  // =========================================================

  function handleViewDetails(event) {
    setDetailsEventId(event.id);
  }

  // =========================================================
  // CERRAR DETALLES DEL EVENTO
  // =========================================================

  function handleCloseDetails() {
    setDetailsEventId(null);
  }

  // =========================================================
  // EDITAR DESDE LA VISTA DE DETALLES
  // =========================================================

  function handleEditFromDetails(event) {
    handleCloseDetails();
    handleEditEvent(event);
  }

  // =========================================================
  // CERRAR FORMULARIO
  // =========================================================

  function handleCloseForm() {
    setIsFormOpen(false);
    setSelectedEvent(null);
  }

  // =========================================================
  // GUARDAR EVENTO
  // =========================================================

  function handleSaveEvent(eventData) {
    if (selectedEvent) {

      // EDICIÓN:
      // Conservamos el identificador del evento
      // y actualizamos únicamente el registro
      // correspondiente.

      setEvents((previousEvents) =>
        previousEvents.map((event) =>
          event.id === selectedEvent.id
            ? {
              ...event,
              ...eventData,
              id: event.id,
            }
            : event
        )
      );

    } else {

      // CREACIÓN:
      // Generamos un identificador temporal.
      // El backend asignará el ID definitivo
      // cuando integremos MongoDB.

      const newEvent = {
        ...eventData,

        id: `EVT-${crypto.randomUUID()}`,

        confirmations: 0,
      };

      setEvents((previousEvents) => [
        ...previousEvents,
        newEvent,
      ]);
    }

    handleCloseForm();
  }

  // =========================================================
  // GESTIÓN DEL ESTADO DE PUBLICACIÓN
  // =========================================================

  // Cambia el estado de publicación de un evento.
  function handleChangePublicationStatus(eventId, newStatus) {
    setEvents((previousEvents) =>
      previousEvents.map((event) =>
        event.id === eventId
          ? {
            ...event,
            publicationStatus: newStatus,
          }
          : event
      )
    );
  }

  // =========================================================
  // RESUMEN DE EVENTOS
  // =========================================================

  // =========================================================
  // RESUMEN DE EVENTOS
  // =========================================================

  const summary = useMemo(() => {
    return {
      total: events.length,

      published: events.filter(
        (event) =>
          event.publicationStatus === "published"
      ).length,

      drafts: events.filter(
        (event) =>
          event.publicationStatus === "draft"
      ).length,

      recurring: events.filter(
        (event) =>
          event.recurrence?.type === "weekly"
      ).length,
    };
  }, [events]);

  // =========================================================
  // BÚSQUEDA Y FILTROS COMBINADOS
  // =========================================================

  const filteredEvents = useMemo(() => {
    const query = search.trim().toLocaleLowerCase("es-CL");

    return events.filter((event) => {
      // Búsqueda textual.
      const searchableText = [
        event.title,
        event.category,
        event.description,
        event.modality,
        event.location,
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(" ")
        .toLocaleLowerCase("es-CL");

      const matchesSearch = searchableText.includes(query);

      // Estado de publicación.
      const matchesStatus =
        statusFilter === "all" ||
        event.publicationStatus === statusFilter;

      // Frecuencia.
      const matchesFrequency =
        frequencyFilter === "all" ||
        event.recurrence?.type === frequencyFilter;

      // Modalidad.
      const matchesModality =
        modalityFilter === "all" ||
        event.modality === modalityFilter;

      return (
        matchesSearch &&
        matchesStatus &&
        matchesFrequency &&
        matchesModality
      );
    });
  }, [
    events,
    search,
    statusFilter,
    frequencyFilter,
    modalityFilter,
  ]);

  // =========================================================
  // INTERFAZ
  // =========================================================

  return (
    <div className="events-management-page">

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* VOLVER AL PANEL */}
      {/* ============================================= */}

      <div className="events-management-page__navigation">
        <Link
          to={adminBasePath}
          className="events-management-page__back"
        >
          <ArrowLeft
            size={17}
            strokeWidth={2}
            aria-hidden="true"
          />

          <span>Volver al panel</span>
        </Link>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* ENCABEZADO */}
      {/* ============================================= */}

      <header className="events-management-page__header">
        <div className="events-management-page__heading">
          <span className="events-management-page__eyebrow">
            COMUNIDAD / ACTIVIDADES
          </span>

          <h1>Gestión de eventos</h1>

          <p>
            Organiza las actividades de la iglesia,
            consulta su publicación y administra
            eventos individuales o recurrentes.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* El formulario se construirá después. */}

        <button
          className="events-management-page__create"
          type="button"
          onClick={handleCreateEvent}
        >
          <CalendarPlus
            size={19}
            aria-hidden="true"
          />

          Nuevo evento
        </button>
      </header>

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* AVISO DE DEMOSTRACIÓN */}
      {/* ============================================= */}

      <div
        className="events-management-page__notice"
        role="note"
      >
        <Info
          size={19}
          aria-hidden="true"
        />

        <div>
          <strong>Vista de demostración</strong>

          <p>
            Los eventos y las estadísticas de esta
            página son ilustrativos. Todavía no
            están conectados con MongoDB.
            La creación y edición se incorporarán
            en los siguientes pasos.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* TARJETAS DE RESUMEN */}
      {/* ============================================= */}

      <section
        className="events-management-page__summary"
        aria-label="Resumen de eventos"
      >
        <article className="events-management-summary-card">
          <div className="events-management-summary-card__icon">
            <CalendarDays size={23} aria-hidden="true" />
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Total de eventos</span>
            <strong>{summary.total}</strong>
            <small>Actividades registradas</small>
          </div>
        </article>

        <article className="events-management-summary-card">
          <div className="events-management-summary-card__icon">
            <CheckCircle2 size={23} aria-hidden="true" />
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Publicados</span>
            <strong>{summary.published}</strong>
            <small>Visibles para la comunidad</small>
          </div>
        </article>

        <article className="events-management-summary-card">
          <div className="events-management-summary-card__icon">
            <FilePenLine size={23} aria-hidden="true" />
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Borradores</span>
            <strong>{summary.drafts}</strong>
            <small>Pendientes de publicación</small>
          </div>
        </article>

        <article className="events-management-summary-card">
          <div className="events-management-summary-card__icon">
            <Repeat2 size={23} aria-hidden="true" />
          </div>

          <div>
            <span>Recurrentes</span>
            <strong>{summary.recurring}</strong>
            <small>Actividades periódicas</small>
          </div>
        </article>
      </section>

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* LISTADO DE EVENTOS */}
      {/* ============================================= */}

      <section className="events-management-page__listing">

        <div className="events-management-page__listing-header">
          <div>
            <h2>Eventos registrados</h2>

            <p>
              Consulta las actividades y su estado
              de publicación.
            </p>
          </div>

          <span className="events-management-page__count">
            {filteredEvents.length} resultados
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* ============================================= */}
        {/* BUSCADOR */}
        {/* ============================================= */}

        <div className="events-management-page__toolbar">
          <div className="events-management-page__search">
            <Search
              size={19}
              aria-hidden="true"
            />

            <input
              type="search"
              value={search}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setSearch(event.target.value)
              }
              placeholder="Buscar por nombre, categoría o modalidad..."
              aria-label="Buscar eventos"
            />
          </div>
        </div>


        {/* FILTROS AVANZADOS */}

        <div className="events-management-page__filters">
          <select
            value={statusFilter}
            onChange={(event) => setStatusFilter(event.target.value)}
            aria-label="Filtrar por publicación"
          >
            <option value="all">Todas las publicaciones</option>
            <option value="published">Publicados</option>
            <option value="draft">Borradores</option>
          </select>

          <select
            value={frequencyFilter}
            onChange={(event) => setFrequencyFilter(event.target.value)}
            aria-label="Filtrar por frecuencia"
          >
            <option value="all">Todas las frecuencias</option>
            <option value="weekly">Recurrentes</option>
            <option value="none">Eventos únicos</option>
          </select>

          <select
            value={modalityFilter}
            onChange={(event) => setModalityFilter(event.target.value)}
            aria-label="Filtrar por modalidad"
          >
            <option value="all">Todas las modalidades</option>
            <option value="Presencial">Presenciales</option>
            <option value="Online">Online</option>
            <option value="Híbrida">Híbridas</option>
          </select>

          <button
            type="button"
            className="events-management-page__clear-filters"
            onClick={handleClearFilters}
          >
            Limpiar
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* ============================================= */}
        {/* TABLA */}
        {/* ============================================= */}

        {filteredEvents.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="events-management-page__table-scroll">
            <table className="events-management-page__table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="col">Evento</th>
                  <th scope="col">Fecha y hora</th>
                  <th scope="col">Modalidad</th>
                  <th scope="col">Frecuencia</th>
                  <th scope="col">Publicación</th>
                  <th scope="col">Asistencia</th>
                  <th scope="col">Acciones</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>

              <tbody>
                {filteredEvents.map((event) => {
                  const isRecurring =
                    event.recurrence.type !== "none";

                  const isPublished =
                    event.publicationStatus === "published";

                  return (
                    <tr key={event.id}>

                      {/* EVENTO */}

                      <td>
                        <div className="events-management-page__event">
                          <strong>{event.title}</strong>
                          <span>{event.category}</span>
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      {/* FECHA Y HORA */}

                      <td>
                        <div className="events-management-page__datetime">
                          <span>
                            <CalendarDays
                              size={15}
                              aria-hidden="true"
                            />

                            {formatEventDate(event.date)}
                          </span>

                          <span>
                            <Clock3
                              size={15}
                              aria-hidden="true"
                            />

                            {event.time} hrs
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      {/* MODALIDAD */}

                      <td>
                        <div className="events-management-page__modality">
                          {event.modality === "Online" ? (
                            <MonitorPlay
                              size={16}
                              aria-hidden="true"
                            />
                          ) : (
                            <MapPin
                              size={16}
                              aria-hidden="true"
                            />
                          )}

                          <span>{event.modality}</span>
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      {/* RECURRENCIA */}

                      <td>
                        <span
                          className={
                            isRecurring
                              ? "events-management-page__frequency events-management-page__frequency--recurring"
                              : "events-management-page__frequency"
                          }
                        >
                          {isRecurring ? (
                            <>
                              <Repeat2
                                size={14}
                                aria-hidden="true"
                              />

                              Semanal
                            </>
                          ) : (
                            "Único"
                          )}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      {/* PUBLICACIÓN */}

                      <td>
                        <span
                          className={
                            isPublished
                              ? "events-management-page__status events-management-page__status--published"
                              : "events-management-page__status events-management-page__status--draft"
                          }
                        >
                          {isPublished
                            ? "Publicado"
                            : "Borrador"}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      {/* CONFIRMACIONES */}

                      <td>
                        <span className="events-management-page__attendance">
                          <UsersRound
                            size={16}
                            aria-hidden="true"
                          />

                          {event.confirmations}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      {/* ============================================= */}
                      {/* ACCIONES DEL EVENTO */}
                      {/* ============================================= */}

                      <td>
                        <div className="events-management-page__actions">

                          {/* VER DETALLES */}

                          <button
                            className="events-management-page__details-button"
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleViewDetails(event)}
                            aria-label={`Ver detalles de ${event.title}`}
                            title="Ver detalles del evento"
                          >
                            <Eye
                              size={16}
                              aria-hidden="true"
                            />

                            Ver detalles
                          </button>

                          <button
                            className="events-management-page__details-button"
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleViewConfirmations(event)}
                            aria-label={`Ver confirmaciones de ${event.title}`}
                          >
                            <UsersRound size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
                            Confirmaciones
                          </button>

                          {/* EDITAR EVENTO */}

                          <button
                            className="events-management-page__edit-button"
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleEditEvent(event)}
                            aria-label={`Editar evento ${event.title}`}
                            title="Editar evento"
                          >
                            <Pencil
                              size={16}
                              aria-hidden="true"
                            />

                            Editar
                          </button>

                          {/* PUBLICAR O PASAR A BORRADOR */}

                          <button
                            className={`events-management-page__publication-button ${isPublished
                              ? "events-management-page__publication-button--draft"
                              : "events-management-page__publication-button--publish"
                              }`}
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() =>
                              handleChangePublicationStatus(
                                event.id,
                                isPublished ? "draft" : "published"
                              )
                            }
                            aria-label={
                              isPublished
                                ? `Pasar a borrador ${event.title}`
                                : `Publicar ${event.title}`
                            }
                          >
                            {isPublished ? "Pasar a borrador" : "Publicar"}
                          </button>

                        </div>
                      </td>

                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="events-management-page__empty">
            <CalendarDays
              size={36}
              strokeWidth={1.5}
              aria-hidden="true"
            />

            <h3>No se encontraron eventos</h3>

            <p>
              Prueba con otro término de búsqueda.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

      </section>

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* NOTA DE PRÓXIMAS FUNCIONALIDADES */}
      {/* ============================================= */}

      <footer className="events-management-page__footer">
        <Repeat2
          size={19}
          aria-hidden="true"
        />

        <div>
          <strong>
            Eventos individuales y recurrentes
          </strong>

          <p>
            El módulo permitirá registrar actividades
            para una fecha específica o configurar
            recurrencias semanales. También incorporará
            formularios de edición, publicación y
            gestión de confirmaciones.
          </p>
        </div>
      </footer>

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* FORMULARIO DE CREACIÓN Y EDICIÓN */}
      {/* ============================================= */}

      <EventFormModal
        isOpen={isFormOpen}
        onClose={handleCloseForm}
        onSave={handleSaveEvent}
        eventToEdit={selectedEvent}
      />

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* VISTA DE DETALLES DEL EVENTO */}
      {/* ============================================= */}

      <EventDetailsModal
        isOpen={Boolean(detailsEvent)}
        event={detailsEvent}
        onClose={handleCloseDetails}
        onEdit={handleEditFromDetails}
      />

      {/* ============================================= */}
      {/* GESTIÓN DE CONFIRMACIONES */}
      {/* ============================================= */}

      <EventConfirmationsModal
        isOpen={Boolean(confirmationsEvent)}
        event={confirmationsEvent}
        onClose={handleCloseConfirmations}
      />

    </div>
  );
}

export default EventsManagement;

//frontend/src/pages/ExpenseManagement.jsx

// Importamos useState para controlar
// la sección seleccionada y el modal.
import { useState } from "react";

// Importamos el componente que contiene el encabezado
// de la página de gestión de gastos.
import ExpenseHeader from "../../components/administration/expenses/ExpenseHeader";

// Importamos el menú interno del módulo.
import ExpenseNavigation from "../../components/administration/expenses/ExpenseNavigation";

// Importamos las tarjetas con los indicadores
// principales de los gastos.
import ExpenseSummary from "../../components/administration/expenses/ExpenseSummary";

// Importamos los gráficos correspondientes
// al análisis de los gastos.
import ExpenseCharts from "../../components/administration/expenses/ExpenseCharts";

// Importamos la sección de gastos próximos.
import UpcomingExpenses from "../../components/administration/expenses/UpcomingExpenses";

// Importamos el historial completo de gastos.
import ExpenseTable from "../../components/administration/expenses/ExpenseTable";

// Importamos el modal visual
// para registrar un nuevo gasto.
import ExpenseFormModal from "../../components/administration/expenses/ExpenseFormModal";

// Importamos los estilos generales de la página.
import "../../styles/pages/administration/ExpenseManagement.css";


// Creamos el componente principal de la página.
function ExpenseManagement() {

  // ---------------------------------------------------------
  // SECCIÓN ACTIVA
  // ---------------------------------------------------------

  // Guardamos qué sección desea visualizar el administrador.
  // Por defecto mostramos el resumen.
  const [activeSection, setActiveSection] = useState("summary");


  // ---------------------------------------------------------
  // MODAL
  // ---------------------------------------------------------

  // Guardamos si el modal de registro
  // se encuentra abierto o cerrado.
  const [isExpenseModalOpen, setIsExpenseModalOpen] =
    useState(false);


  // Retornamos la estructura base de la página.
  return (
    <main className="expense-management">

      {/* Contenedor central de toda la página. */}
      <div className="expense-management__container">

        {/* Mostramos el encabezado.
            Cuando se presiona Registrar gasto,
            abrimos el modal. */}
        <ExpenseHeader
          onRegisterExpense={() =>
            setIsExpenseModalOpen(true)
          }
        />


        {/* Mostramos el menú interno
            correspondiente al módulo financiero. */}
        <ExpenseNavigation
          activeSection={activeSection}
          onSectionChange={setActiveSection}
        />


        {/* ================================================= */}
        {/* CONTENIDO DINÁMICO */}
        {/* ================================================= */}

        <div className="expense-management__content">

          {/* Mostramos el resumen solamente
              cuando esta sección se encuentra activa. */}
          {activeSection === "summary" && (
            <ExpenseSummary />
          )}


          {/* Mostramos los gráficos solamente
              cuando el usuario selecciona Análisis. */}
          {activeSection === "charts" && (
            <ExpenseCharts />
          )}


          {/* Mostramos los gastos pendientes
              solamente cuando corresponde. */}
          {activeSection === "upcoming" && (
            <UpcomingExpenses />
          )}


          {/* Mostramos la tabla histórica
              solamente al seleccionar Historial. */}
          {activeSection === "history" && (
            <ExpenseTable />
          )}

        </div>

      </div>


      {/* El modal se encuentra fuera del contenedor
          porque debe cubrir toda la pantalla. */}
      <ExpenseFormModal
        isOpen={isExpenseModalOpen}
        onClose={() =>
          setIsExpenseModalOpen(false)
        }
      />

    </main>
  );
}


// Exportamos el componente.
export default ExpenseManagement;

// =========================================================
// COMPONENTE PRINCIPAL
// =========================================================

function MembersManagement() {

    // =====================================================
    // NAVEGACIÓN
    // =====================================================

    const navigate = useNavigate();

    function handleGoBack() {
        navigate(-1);
    }

    // =====================================================
    // ESTADOS GENERALES
    // =====================================================

    // Datos compartidos por los componentes de la página.

    const [members] = useState(DEMO_MEMBERS);

    // Miembro seleccionado desde el directorio o cumpleaños.

    const [selectedMember, setSelectedMember] = useState(null);

    function handleCloseMemberProfile() {
        setSelectedMember(null);
    }

    // =====================================================
    // MIEMBROS REGISTRADOS
    // =====================================================

    // Excluimos las cuentas administrativas del directorio.

    const registeredMembers = useMemo(() => {
        return members.filter(
            (member) => member.role === "member"
        );
    }, [members]);

    // =====================================================
    // ESTADÍSTICAS GENERALES
    // =====================================================

    const summary = useMemo(() => {

        const now = new Date();

        const currentMonth = now.getMonth();
        const currentYear = now.getFullYear();

        // Total de miembros.
        const total = registeredMembers.length;

        // Miembros con cuenta habilitada.
        const active = registeredMembers.filter(
            (member) => member.status === "active"
        ).length;

        // Cumpleaños durante el mes actual.
        const birthdays = registeredMembers.filter((member) => {

            if (!member.birthDate) return false;

            const month = Number(
                member.birthDate.split("-")[1]
            ) - 1;

            return month === currentMonth;

        }).length;

        // Miembros registrados durante el mes actual.
        const newMembers = registeredMembers.filter((member) => {

            if (!member.registeredAt) return false;

            const [year, month] =
                member.registeredAt.split("-").map(Number);

            return (
                year === currentYear &&
                month === currentMonth + 1
            );

        }).length;

        return {
            total,
            active,
            birthdays,
            newMembers,
        };

    }, [registeredMembers]);

    // =====================================================
    // INTERFAZ PRINCIPAL
    // =====================================================

    return (
        <main className="members-management">

            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* ENCABEZADO PRINCIPAL */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            <header className="members-management__header">

                <div className="members-management__heading">

                    <span className="members-management__eyebrow">
                        GESTIÓN DE LA COMUNIDAD
                    </span>

                    <h1>Miembros de la iglesia</h1>

                    <p>
                        Consulta la información de los miembros
                        registrados y facilita el acompañamiento
                        de nuestra comunidad.
                    </p>

                    <button
                        type="button"
                        className="members-management__back"
                        onClick={handleGoBack}
                    >
                        <ArrowLeft
                            size={17}
                            aria-hidden="true"
                        />
                        Volver
                    </button>

                </div>

                <div className="members-management__header-icon">

                    <UsersRound
                        size={29}
                        strokeWidth={1.7}
                        aria-hidden="true"
                    />

                </div>

            </header>

            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* RESUMEN DE MIEMBROS */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            <MembersSummary
                summary={summary}
            />

            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* CUMPLEAÑOS DE LA COMUNIDAD */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            <MembersBirthdays
                members={registeredMembers}
                onViewMember={setSelectedMember}
            />

            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* DIRECTORIO DE MIEMBROS */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            <MembersDirectory
                members={registeredMembers}
                onViewMember={setSelectedMember}
            />

            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* AVISO INFORMATIVO */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            <div className="members-management__notice">

                <ShieldCheck
                    size={19}
                    aria-hidden="true"
                />

                <p>
                    Este directorio es una vista administrativa
                    de consulta. La modificación de cuentas,
                    roles y permisos corresponde a la sección
                    Gestión de usuarios.
                </p>

            </div>

            {/* =========================================== */}
            {/* MODAL DE PERFIL DEL MIEMBRO */}
            {/* =========================================== */}

            {selectedMember && (
                <MemberDetailsModal
                    member={selectedMember}
                    onClose={handleCloseMemberProfile}
                />
            )}

        </main>
    );
}

export default MembersManagement;

import {
    ArrowLeft,
    CheckCircle2,
    Clock3,

    HandHeart,

    Search,

    UsersRound,
} from "lucide-react";
import { Link, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";

import "../../styles/pages/administration/PrayerRequestsManagement.css";

import PrayerRequestDetailsModal from "../../components/administration/prayers/PrayerRequestDetailsModal";

// =========================================================
// DATOS DE DEMOSTRACIÓN
// =========================================================

        label: "Pendiente",
        className: "pending",
    },
    praying: {

    },

    },
    completed: {
        label: "Finalizada",
        className: "completed",
    },

// =========================================================
// FORMATO DE FECHAS
// =========================================================

function formatDate(dateString) {
    if (!dateString) return "Sin fecha";

    const date = new Date(`${dateString}T12:00:00Z`);

    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
        return "Fecha no disponible";
    }

    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-CL", {
        day: "2-digit",
        month: "short",
        year: "numeric",
        timeZone: "UTC",
    }).format(date);
}

// =========================================================
// COMPONENTE PRINCIPAL
// =========================================================

function PrayerRequestsManagement() {
    const location = useLocation();

    const isSuperAdminPanel =
        location.pathname.startsWith("/super-admin");

    const adminBasePath = isSuperAdminPanel
        ? "/super-admin"
        : "/admin";

    // -------------------------------------------------------
    // ESTADOS
    // -------------------------------------------------------

    const [requests, setRequests] = useState(DEMO_PRAYER_REQUESTS);

    // =========================================================
    // MODAL DE DETALLES
    // =========================================================

    // Guardamos el identificador de la petición seleccionada.
    const [selectedRequestId, setSelectedRequestId] =
        useState(null);

    // Obtenemos siempre la versión actualizada de la petición.
    const selectedRequest =
        requests.find(
            (request) => request.id === selectedRequestId
        ) ?? null;

    // Abrir detalles.
    function handleViewDetails(request) {
        setSelectedRequestId(request.id);
    }

    // Cerrar detalles.
    function handleCloseDetails() {
        setSelectedRequestId(null);
    }

    // Guardar estado y observación de seguimiento.
    function handleSaveDetails(requestId, changes) {
        setRequests((previousRequests) =>
            previousRequests.map((request) =>
                request.id === requestId
                    ? {
                        ...request,
                        status: changes.status,
                        followUpNote: changes.followUpNote,
                    }
                    : request
            )
        );

        setSelectedRequestId(null);
    }

    const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
    const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("all");
    const [privacyFilter, setPrivacyFilter] = useState("all");
    const [categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter] = useState("all");

    // -------------------------------------------------------
    // RESUMEN
    // -------------------------------------------------------

    const summary = useMemo(() => {
        return {
            total: requests.length,

            pending: requests.filter(
                (request) => request.status === "pending"
            ).length,

            praying: requests.filter(
                (request) => request.status === "praying"
            ).length,

            answered: requests.filter(
                (request) => request.status === "answered"
            ).length,

            completed: requests.filter(
                (request) => request.status === "completed"
            ).length,
        };
    }, [requests]);

    // -------------------------------------------------------
    // CATEGORÍAS DISPONIBLES
    // -------------------------------------------------------

    const categories = useMemo(() => {
        return [...new Set(requests.map((request) => request.category))]
            .sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, "es"));
    }, [requests]);

    // -------------------------------------------------------
    // FILTRADO DE PETICIONES
    // -------------------------------------------------------

    const filteredRequests = useMemo(() => {
        const normalizedSearch = search.trim().toLowerCase();

        return requests.filter((request) => {
            // No buscamos dentro de descripciones privadas
            // para evitar exponer su contenido en esta vista.
            const searchableText = [
                request.id,
                request.title,
                request.category,
                request.requester,
                request.anonymous ? "Anónimo" : "",
            ]
                .filter(Boolean)
                .join(" ")
                .toLowerCase();

            const matchesSearch =
                !normalizedSearch ||
                searchableText.includes(normalizedSearch);

            const matchesStatus =
                statusFilter === "all" ||
                request.status === statusFilter;

            const matchesPrivacy =
                privacyFilter === "all" ||
                request.privacy === privacyFilter;

            const matchesCategory =
                categoryFilter === "all" ||
                request.category === categoryFilter;

            return (
                matchesSearch &&
                matchesStatus &&
                matchesPrivacy &&
                matchesCategory
            );
        });
    }, [
        requests,
        search,
        statusFilter,
        privacyFilter,
        categoryFilter,
    ]);

    // -------------------------------------------------------
    // LIMPIAR FILTROS
    // -------------------------------------------------------

    function handleClearFilters() {
        setSearch("");
        setStatusFilter("all");
        setPrivacyFilter("all");
        setCategoryFilter("all");
    }

    const hasActiveFilters =
        search.trim() !== "" ||
        statusFilter !== "all" ||
        privacyFilter !== "all" ||
        categoryFilter !== "all";

    // -------------------------------------------------------
    // INTERFAZ
    // -------------------------------------------------------

    return (
        <div className="prayer-management-page">

            {/* ============================================= */}
            {/* ENCABEZADO */}
            {/* ============================================= */}

            <header className="prayer-management-page__header">

                <div className="prayer-management-page__heading">

                    <Link
                        to={adminBasePath}
                        className="prayer-management-page__back"
                    >
                        <ArrowLeft size={17} aria-hidden="true" />
                        Volver al panel
                    </Link>

                    <span className="prayer-management-page__eyebrow">

            {/* ============================================= */}

            <div className="prayer-management-page__privacy-notice">

                <ShieldCheck size={21} aria-hidden="true" />

                <p>

                    </div>

                    <strong>{summary.pending}</strong>
                    <small>Esperando atención</small>

                </article>

                <article className="prayer-management-page__summary-card">

                    <strong>{summary.praying}</strong>
                    <small>Actualmente en seguimiento</small>

                </article>

                <article className="prayer-management-page__summary-card">

                    <div className="prayer-management-page__summary-icon">

                    </div>

                    <strong>{summary.answered}</strong>
                    <small>Con respuesta registrada</small>

                </article>

            </section>

            {/* ============================================= */}

            {/* ============================================= */}

                <div className="prayer-management-page__section-header">

                    <div>
                        <h2>Solicitudes recibidas</h2>

                        <p>

                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <span className="prayer-management-page__result-count">
                        {filteredRequests.length} resultados
                    </span>

                </div>

                {/* =========================================== */}
                {/* FILTROS */}
                {/* =========================================== */}

                <div className="prayer-management-page__filters">

                    <label className="prayer-management-page__search">

                        <Search size={19} aria-hidden="true" />

                        <input
                            type="search"
                            placeholder="Buscar por título, nombre o código..."
                            aria-label="Buscar peticiones"
                            value={search}
                            onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}
                        />

                    </label>

                    <select
                        aria-label="Filtrar por estado"
                        value={statusFilter}
                        onChange={(event) => setStatusFilter(event.target.value)}
                    >
                        <option value="all">Todos los estados</option>
                        <option value="pending">Pendiente</option>
                        <option value="praying">En oración</option>
                        <option value="answered">Respondida</option>
                        <option value="completed">Finalizada</option>
                    </select>

                    <select
                        aria-label="Filtrar por privacidad"
                        value={privacyFilter}
                        onChange={(event) => setPrivacyFilter(event.target.value)}
                    >
                        <option value="all">Toda la privacidad</option>
                        <option value="community">Comunitaria</option>
                        <option value="private">Privada</option>
                    </select>

                    <select
                        aria-label="Filtrar por categoría"
                        value={categoryFilter}
                        onChange={(event) => setCategoryFilter(event.target.value)}
                    >
                        <option value="all">Todas las categorías</option>

                        {categories.map((category) => (
                            <option key={category} value={category}>
                                {category}
                            </option>
                        ))}

                    </select>

                </div>

                {hasActiveFilters && (
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        className="prayer-management-page__clear-filters"
                        onClick={handleClearFilters}
                    >
                        Limpiar filtros
                    </button>
                )}

                {/* =========================================== */}
                {/* TABLA */}
                {/* =========================================== */}

                <div className="prayer-management-page__table-wrapper">

                    <table className="prayer-management-page__table">

                        <thead>
                            <tr>
                                <th>Petición</th>
                                <th>Solicitante</th>
                                <th>Fecha</th>
                                <th>Privacidad</th>
                                <th>Estado</th>
                                <th>Acciones</th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>

                        <tbody>

                            {filteredRequests.map((request) => {

                                const status =
                                    STATUS_CONFIG[request.status] ||
                                    STATUS_CONFIG.pending;

                                return (
                                    <tr key={request.id}>

                                        <td>
                                            <div className="prayer-management-page__request">

                                                <span className="prayer-management-page__request-code">
                                                    {request.id}
                                                </span>

                                                <strong>{request.title}</strong>

                                                <small>{request.category}</small>

                                            </div>
                                        </td>

                                        <td>
                                            <div className="prayer-management-page__requester">
                                                <strong>{request.requester}</strong>

                                                {request.anonymous && (
                                                    <span className="prayer-management-page__anonymous-label">
                                                        <LockKeyhole size={12} aria-hidden="true" />
                                                        Anónimo para miembros
                                                    </span>
                                                )}
                                            </div>
                                        </td>

                                        <td>{formatDate(request.date)}</td>

                                        <td>

                                            <span
                                                className={`prayer-management-page__privacy-badge prayer-management-page__privacy-badge--${request.privacy}`}
                                            >
                                                {request.privacy === "private" ? (
                                                    <>
                                                        <LockKeyhole
                                                            size={13}
                                                            aria-hidden="true"
                                                        />
                                                        Privada
                                                    </>
                                                ) : (
                                                    "Comunitaria"
                                                )}
                                            </span>

                                        </td>

                                        <td>

                                            <span
                                                className={`prayer-management-page__status prayer-management-page__status--${status.className}`}
                                            >
                                                {status.label}
                                            </span>

                                        </td>

                                        <td>

                                       

                                            <button
                                                type="button"
                                                className="prayer-management-page__details-button"
                                                onClick={() => handleViewDetails(request)}
                                                aria-label={`Ver detalles de la petición ${request.id}`}
                                                title="Ver detalles de la petición"
                                            >
                                                <Eye size={16} aria-hidden="true" />
                                                Ver detalles
                                            </button>

                                        </td>

                                    </tr>
                                );
                            })}

                            {filteredRequests.length === 0 && (
                                <tr>
                                    <td
                                        colSpan={6}
                                        className="prayer-management-page__empty"
                                    >
                                        No se encontraron peticiones con los
                                        filtros seleccionados.
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            )}

                        </tbody>

                    </table>

                </div>

                <footer className="prayer-management-page__footer">

                    <LockKeyhole size={16} aria-hidden="true" />

                    <span>
                        Los datos mostrados son demostrativos.
                        El acceso real deberá estar protegido mediante
                        autenticación y autorización desde el servidor.
                    </span>

                </footer>

            </section>

            {/* ============================================= */}
            {/* MODAL DE DETALLES Y SEGUIMIENTO */}
            {/* ============================================= */}

            <PrayerRequestDetailsModal
                isOpen={Boolean(selectedRequest)}
                request={selectedRequest}
                onClose={handleCloseDetails}
                onSave={handleSaveDetails}
            />

        </div>
    );
}

export default PrayerRequestsManagement;

import {
    useState,
    useCallback,
} from "react";

import {
    LockKeyhole,
} from "lucide-react";


// =========================================================
// COMPONENTES
// =========================================================

import UsersHeader from "../../components/administration/users/UsersHeader";

import UsersSummary from "../../components/administration/users/UsersSummary";

import UsersFilters from "../../components/administration/users/UsersFilters";

import UsersTable from "../../components/administration/users/UsersTable";

import UserDetailsModal from "../../components/administration/users/UserDetailsModal";

import UserEditModal from "../../components/administration/users/UserEditModal";


// =========================================================
// ESTILOS
// =========================================================


import "../../styles/pages/administration/UsersManagement.css";



// =========================================================
// PÁGINA PRINCIPAL
// =========================================================

function UsersManagement() {

    // =========================================================
    // ESTADOS DE FILTRADO
    // =========================================================

    const [search, setSearch] = useState("");

    const [role, setRole] = useState("all");

    const [status, setStatus] = useState("all");


    // =========================================================
    // DATOS DE USUARIOS
    // =========================================================

    // Posteriormente reemplazaremos este arreglo
    // con los usuarios obtenidos desde el backend.

    const users = [];


    // =========================================================
    // USUARIO SELECCIONADO
    // =========================================================

    // Al seleccionar "Ver usuario" en la tabla,
    // guardaremos el objeto de la cuenta.

    const [selectedUser, setSelectedUser] = useState(null);


    // =========================================================
    // RESTABLECER FILTROS
    // =========================================================

    const handleClearFilters = () => {

        setSearch("");

        setRole("all");

        setStatus("all");

    };


    // =========================================================
    // ABRIR MODAL
    // =========================================================

    const handleViewUser = useCallback((user) => {

        setSelectedUser(user);

    }, []);


    // =========================================================
    // CERRAR MODAL
    // =========================================================

    const handleCloseModal = useCallback(() => {

        setSelectedUser(null);

    }, []);


    // =========================================================
    // EDICIÓN DE USUARIOS
    // =========================================================

    const [editingUser, setEditingUser] = useState(null);

    // Abrir el formulario con el usuario seleccionado.
    const handleEditUser = useCallback((user) => {
        setEditingUser(user);
    }, []);

    // Cerrar el formulario.
    const handleCloseEditModal = useCallback(() => {
        setEditingUser(null);
    }, []);



    // =========================================================
    // INTERFAZ
    // =========================================================

    return (

        <div className="users-management">


            {/* ================================================= */}
            {/* ENCABEZADO */}
            {/* ================================================= */}

            <UsersHeader
                backPath="/super-admin"
            />


            {/* ================================================= */}
            {/* CONTENIDO PRINCIPAL */}
            {/* ================================================= */}

            <div className="users-management__content">


                {/* ================================================= */}
                {/* RESUMEN DE USUARIOS */}
                {/* ================================================= */}

                <UsersSummary />


                {/* ================================================= */}
                {/* LISTADO DE USUARIOS */}
                {/* ================================================= */}

                <section
                    className="users-management__section"
                    aria-labelledby="users-list-title"
                >

                    <div className="users-management__section-heading">

                        <div>

                            <h2 id="users-list-title">
                                Usuarios registrados
                            </h2>

                            <p>
                                Consulta las cuentas, utiliza filtros
                                y accede a las herramientas de gestión.
                            </p>

                        </div>

                    </div>


                    {/* ============================================= */}
                    {/* BUSCADOR Y FILTROS */}
                    {/* ============================================= */}

                    <UsersFilters
                        search={search}
                        role={role}
                        status={status}
                        onSearchChange={setSearch}
                        onRoleChange={setRole}
                        onStatusChange={setStatus}
                        onClear={handleClearFilters}
                    />


                    {/* ============================================= */}
                    {/* TABLA DE USUARIOS */}
                    {/* ============================================= */}

                    <UsersTable
                        users={users}
                        loading={false}
                        search={search}
                        role={role}
                        status={status}
                        onViewUser={handleViewUser}
                        onEditUser={handleEditUser}
                    />

                </section>


                {/* ================================================= */}
                {/* INFORMACIÓN DE SEGURIDAD */}
                {/* ================================================= */}

                <aside className="users-management__security">

                    <div className="users-management__security-icon">

                        <LockKeyhole
                            size={21}
                            strokeWidth={1.8}
                            aria-hidden="true"
                        />

                    </div>


                    <div className="users-management__security-content">

                        <strong>
                            Administración protegida
                        </strong>

                        <p>
                            Las operaciones sobre cuentas y permisos
                            estarán sujetas a controles de autorización.
                            Los cambios administrativos deberán quedar
                            registrados en el historial de actividad
                            del sistema.
                        </p>

                    </div>

                </aside>

            </div>


            {/* ================================================= */}
            {/* MODAL DE DETALLES */}
            {/* ================================================= */}

            {selectedUser && (

                <UserDetailsModal
                    user={selectedUser}
                    onClose={handleCloseModal}
                />

            )}


            {/* ================================================= */}
            {/* MODAL DE EDICIÓN */}
            {/* ================================================= */}

            {editingUser && (

                <UserEditModal
                    user={editingUser}
                    branches={[]}
                    onClose={handleCloseEditModal}
                />

            )}


        </div>
    );
}


// =========================================================
// EXPORTACIÓN
// =========================================================

export default UsersManagement;

                  <p>Un punto de partida para estudiar, reflexionar y orar.</p>
                </div>
                <Link className="member-text-link" to="/recursos">Todos los recursos <ArrowRight size={17} aria-hidden="true" /></Link>
              </div>

              <div className="member-resources">
                {resourceLinks.map((resource) => {
                  const ResourceIcon = resource.icon;
                  return (
                    <Link className="member-resource" key={resource.to} to={resource.to}>
                      <span className="member-resource__icon"><ResourceIcon size={23} strokeWidth={1.8} aria-hidden="true" /></span>
                      <strong>{resource.title}</strong>
                      <span>{resource.description}</span>
                      <span className="member-resource__arrow">Explorar <ArrowRight size={16} aria-hidden="true" /></span>
                    </Link>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </section>
          </div>

          <aside className="member-grid__aside" aria-label="Información personal y comunidad">
            <section className="member-profile" id="mi-perfil" aria-labelledby="member-profile-title">
              <div className="member-profile__heading"><span className="member-profile__icon"><UserRound size={22} aria-hidden="true" /></span><div><span>Cuenta de miembro</span><h2 id="member-profile-title">Mi perfil</h2></div></div>
              {user ? (
                <dl className="member-profile__details">
                  <div><dt>Nombre</dt><dd>{fullName || "Sin registrar"}</dd></div>
                  <div><dt>Correo</dt><dd className="member-profile__email">{user.email || "Sin registrar"}</dd></div>
                  <div><dt>Sucursal principal</dt><dd>{primaryBranch || "Por confirmar"}</dd></div>
                </dl>
              ) : (
                <p className="member-profile__empty">Tus datos aparecerán aquí cuando conectemos esta página con tu sesión verificada.</p>
              )}
              <p className="member-profile__note"><ShieldCheck size={17} aria-hidden="true" /> Tu información personal se mostrará solo en tu sesión.</p>
            </section>

            <section className="member-community" aria-labelledby="member-community-title">
              <div className="member-community__icon"><HeartHandshake size={26} aria-hidden="true" /></div>
              <h2 id="member-community-title">¿Necesitas conversar?</h2>
              <p>Queremos acompañarte. Escríbenos si tienes una consulta o necesitas orientación.</p>
              <Link to="/contacto">Ir a contacto <ArrowRight size={17} aria-hidden="true" /></Link>
            </section>

            <div className="member-help"><Mail size={19} aria-hidden="true" /><p>¿Buscas algo más? <Link to="/recursos/preguntas-frecuentes">Revisa las preguntas frecuentes</Link>.</p></div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      </main>

      <footer className="member-footer"><div className="member-container"><span>© {new Date().getFullYear()} Iglesia El Ancla</span><Link to="/">Volver al inicio</Link></div></footer>
    </div>
  );
}

export default MemberDashboard;

import { useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

import {
    ArrowLeft,
    CalendarDays,
    MapPin,
} from "lucide-react";

import EventAttendanceCard from "../../components/member/events/EventAttendanceCard";

import "../../styles/pages/member/MemberEvents.css";

// =========================================================
// EVENTOS TEMPORALES
// =========================================================

const DEMO_MEMBER_EVENTS = [
    {
        id: "EVT-004",
        title: "Encuentro de jóvenes",
        date: "2026-10-17",
        time: "18:00",
        location: "Iglesia El Ancla",
        modality: "Presencial",
        requiresConfirmation: true,
    },
    {
        id: "EVT-005",
        title: "Taller para niños",
        date: "2026-10-24",
        time: "16:00",
        location: "Iglesia El Ancla",
        modality: "Presencial",
        requiresConfirmation: true,
    },
];

// =========================================================
// COMPONENTE PRINCIPAL
// =========================================================

function MemberEvents() {

    // =========================================================
    // NAVEGACIÓN
    // =========================================================

    const navigate = useNavigate();

    // Regresa a la página anterior del historial.
    // No depende del rol del usuario.
    function handleGoBack() {
        navigate(-1);
    }

    // =========================================================
    // CONFIRMACIONES TEMPORALES
    // =========================================================

    // Almacena las confirmaciones realizadas
    // durante la sesión actual del componente.
    const [confirmedEventIds, setConfirmedEventIds] =
        useState([]);

    // Confirma la participación en un evento.
    function handleConfirm(event) {
        setConfirmedEventIds((previous) => {

            // Evitamos registrar confirmaciones duplicadas.
            if (previous.includes(event.id)) {
                return previous;
            }

            return [...previous, event.id];
        });
    }

    // Cancela la confirmación de participación.
    function handleCancel(event) {
        setConfirmedEventIds((previous) =>
            previous.filter((id) => id !== event.id)
        );
    }

    // =========================================================
    // INTERFAZ
    // =========================================================

    return (
        <main className="member-events-page">

            {/* ============================================= */}
            {/* NAVEGACIÓN DE REGRESO */}
            {/* ============================================= */}

            <div className="member-events-page__navigation">
                <button
                    type="button"
                    className="member-events-page__back"
                    onClick={handleGoBack}
                >
                    <ArrowLeft
                        size={17}
                        strokeWidth={2}
                        aria-hidden="true"
                    />

                    <span>Volver</span>
                </button>
            </div>

            {/* ============================================= */}
            {/* ENCABEZADO */}
            {/* ============================================= */}

            <header className="member-events-page__header">
                <span>MI COMUNIDAD</span>

                <h1>Mis actividades</h1>

                <p>
                    Conoce las próximas actividades de Iglesia
                    El Ancla y administra tu participación.
                </p>
            </header>

            {/* ============================================= */}
            {/* AVISO DE DEMOSTRACIÓN */}
            {/* ============================================= */}

            <div className="member-events-page__notice">
                Vista de demostración. Las confirmaciones
                todavía no se guardan de forma permanente.
            </div>

            {/* ============================================= */}
            {/* LISTADO DE ACTIVIDADES */}
            {/* ============================================= */}

            <section className="member-events-page__grid">
                {DEMO_MEMBER_EVENTS.map((event) => (
                    <article
                        className="member-events-page__event"
                        key={event.id}
                    >
                        <div className="member-events-page__event-info">

                            <span className="member-events-page__category">
                                ACTIVIDAD DE LA COMUNIDAD
                            </span>

                            <h2>{event.title}</h2>

                            <p>
                                <CalendarDays
                                    size={17}
                                    aria-hidden="true"
                                />

                                {event.date} · {event.time} hrs
                            </p>

                            <p>
                                <MapPin
                                    size={17}
                                    aria-hidden="true"
                                />

                                {event.location}
                            </p>
                        </div>

                        <EventAttendanceCard
                            event={event}
                            isConfirmed={confirmedEventIds.includes(
                                event.id
                            )}
                            onConfirm={handleConfirm}
                            onCancel={handleCancel}
                        />
                    </article>
                ))}
            </section>
        </main>
    );
}

export default MemberEvents;
