// =========================================================
// FUNCIONES AUXILIARES
// =========================================================

function formatDate(dateString) {
    if (!dateString) return "Sin fecha";

    const date = new Date(`${dateString}T12:00:00Z`);

    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
        return "Fecha no disponible";
    }

    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-CL", {
        day: "2-digit",
        month: "short",
        year: "numeric",
        timeZone: "UTC",
    }).format(date);
}

function getStatus(request) {
    return (
        STATUS_CONFIG[request.status] ||
        STATUS_CONFIG.pending
    );
}

// =========================================================
// TARJETA DE PETICIÓN
// =========================================================

function PrayerCard({ request, currentMemberId, onView }) {
    const isOwner = request.memberId === currentMemberId;

    const requesterName =
        request.anonymous && !isOwner
            ? "Miembro anónimo"
            : request.requester;

    const status = getStatus(request);

    return (
        <article className="member-prayer__card">

            <div className="member-prayer__card-top">

                <span className="member-prayer__category">
                    {request.category}
                </span>

                <span
                    className={`member-prayer__status member-prayer__status--${status.className}`}
                >
                    {status.label}
                </span>

            </div>

            <h3>{request.title}</h3>

            <p className="member-prayer__card-description">
                {request.description}
            </p>

            <div className="member-prayer__card-meta">

                <span>
                    <Users size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
                    {requesterName}
                </span>

                <span>
                    <CalendarDays size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
                    {formatDate(request.date)}
                </span>

            </div>

            <div className="member-prayer__card-bottom">

                <span className="member-prayer__visibility-label">
                    {request.visibility === "private" ? (
                        <>
                            <LockKeyhole size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
                            Privada
                        </>
                    ) : (
                        <>
                            <Eye size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
                            Comunitaria
                        </>
                    )}
                </span>

                <button
                    type="button"
                    className="member-prayer__view-button"
                    onClick={() => onView(request)}
                >
                    Ver petición
                    <Eye size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
                </button>

            </div>

        </article>
    );
}

// =========================================================
// COMPONENTE PRINCIPAL
// =========================================================

function MemberPrayerRequests() {

    // Permite navegar entre páginas.
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    // Regresa a la página anterior.
    const handleGoBack = () => {
        navigate(-1);
    };
    const [requests, setRequests] = useState(DEMO_REQUESTS);

    const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState("community");
    const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
    const [categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter] = useState("all");

    const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
    const [selectedRequest, setSelectedRequest] = useState(null);

    const [form, setForm] = useState({
        title: "",
        description: "",
        category: "",
        visibility: "community",
        anonymous: false,
    });

    const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState("");

    const currentMemberId = DEMO_CURRENT_MEMBER.id;

    // -------------------------------------------------------
    // PETICIONES PERSONALES
    // -------------------------------------------------------

    const myRequests = useMemo(() => {
        return requests.filter(
            (request) => request.memberId === currentMemberId
        );
    }, [requests, currentMemberId]);

    // -------------------------------------------------------
    // PETICIONES COMUNITARIAS
    // -------------------------------------------------------

    const communityRequests = useMemo(() => {
        return requests.filter(
            (request) => request.visibility === "community"
        );
    }, [requests]);

    // -------------------------------------------------------
    // ESTADÍSTICAS PERSONALES
    // -------------------------------------------------------

    const summary = useMemo(() => {
        return {
            total: myRequests.length,

            active: myRequests.filter(
                (request) =>
                    request.status === "pending" ||
                    request.status === "praying"
            ).length,

            answered: myRequests.filter(
                (request) => request.status === "answered"
            ).length,
        };
    }, [myRequests]);

    // -------------------------------------------------------
    // FILTROS
    // -------------------------------------------------------

    const filteredRequests = useMemo(() => {
        const source =
            activeTab === "mine"
                ? myRequests
                : communityRequests;

        const normalizedSearch = search.trim().toLowerCase();

        return source.filter((request) => {
            const matchesSearch =
                !normalizedSearch ||
                request.title.toLowerCase().includes(normalizedSearch) ||
                request.description.toLowerCase().includes(normalizedSearch);

            const matchesCategory =
                categoryFilter === "all" ||
                request.category === categoryFilter;

            return matchesSearch && matchesCategory;
        });
    }, [
        activeTab,
        myRequests,
        communityRequests,
        search,
        categoryFilter,
    ]);

    // -------------------------------------------------------
    // ACTUALIZAR FORMULARIO
    // -------------------------------------------------------

    function handleFormChange(event) {
        const { name, value, type, checked } = event.target;

        setForm((previous) => ({
            ...previous,
            [name]: type === "checkbox" ? checked : value,
        }));
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------
    // CREAR PETICIÓN DEMOSTRATIVA
    // -------------------------------------------------------

    function handleSubmit(event) {
        event.preventDefault();

        const title = form.title.trim();
        const description = form.description.trim();

        if (!title || !description || !form.category) {
            return;
        }

        const newRequest = {
            id: `PR-DEMO-${Date.now()}`,

            // En producción este valor vendrá de la sesión
            // autenticada, validada por el backend.
            memberId: currentMemberId,
            requester: DEMO_CURRENT_MEMBER.name,

            title,
            description,
            category: form.category,
            visibility: form.visibility,
            anonymous: form.anonymous,

            status: "pending",
            date: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
        };

        setRequests((previous) => [
            newRequest,
            ...previous,
        ]);

        setForm({
            title: "",
            description: "",
            category: "",
            visibility: "community",
            anonymous: false,
        });

        setShowForm(false);
        setActiveTab("mine");
        setSearch("");
        setCategoryFilter("all");

        setSuccessMessage(
            "Tu petición fue registrada en esta demostración."
        );
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------
    // ABRIR FORMULARIO
    // -------------------------------------------------------

    function handleOpenForm() {
        setSuccessMessage("");
        setShowForm(true);
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------
    // INTERFAZ
    // -------------------------------------------------------

    return (
        <main className="member-prayer">

            <div className="member-prayer__container">

                {/* ============================================= */}
                {/* NAVEGACIÓN DE REGRESO */}
                {/* ============================================= */}

                <nav
                    className="member-prayer__navigation"
                    aria-label="Navegación de regreso"
                >
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        className="member-prayer__back-button"
                        onClick={handleGoBack}
                    >
                        <ArrowLeft
                            size={18}
                            strokeWidth={2}
                            aria-hidden="true"
                        />

                        <span>Volver</span>
                    </button>
                </nav>

                {/* ============================================= */}
                {/* ENCABEZADO */}
                {/* ============================================= */}

                <header className="member-prayer__header">

                    <div className="member-prayer__header-content">

                        <span className="member-prayer__eyebrow">
                            COMUNIDAD EL ANCLA
                        </span>

                        <h1>
                            Peticiones de <span>oración</span>
                        </h1>

                        <p>
                            Un espacio para compartir nuestras cargas,
                            acompañarnos en oración y fortalecer
                            nuestra comunidad de fe.
                        </p>

                        <button
                            type="button"
                            className="member-prayer__primary-button"
                            onClick={handleOpenForm}
                        >
                            <Plus size={19} aria-hidden="true" />
                            Nueva petición
                        </button>

                    </div>

                    <div className="member-prayer__header-decoration">
                        <HandHeart size={65} strokeWidth={1.3} />
                    </div>

                </header>

                {/* ============================================= */}
                {/* MENSAJE DE CONFIRMACIÓN */}
                {/* ============================================= */}

                {successMessage && (
                    <div
                        className="member-prayer__success"
                        role="status"
                    >
                        <CheckCircle2 size={20} aria-hidden="true" />
                        {successMessage}
                    </div>
                )}

                {/* ============================================= */}
                {/* RESUMEN PERSONAL */}
                {/* ============================================= */}

                <section
                    className="member-prayer__summary"
                    aria-label="Resumen de mis peticiones"
                >

                    <article className="member-prayer__summary-card">
                        <div className="member-prayer__summary-icon">
                            <HandHeart size={22} />
                        </div>
                        <span>Mis peticiones</span>
                        <strong>{summary.total}</strong>
                        <small>Solicitudes realizadas</small>
                    </article>

                    <article className="member-prayer__summary-card">
                        <div className="member-prayer__summary-icon">
                            <Clock3 size={22} />
                        </div>
                        <span>En seguimiento</span>
                        <strong>{summary.active}</strong>
                        <small>Pendientes o en oración</small>
                    </article>

                    <article className="member-prayer__summary-card">
                        <div className="member-prayer__summary-icon">
                            <CheckCircle2 size={22} />
                        </div>
                        <span>Respondidas</span>
                        <strong>{summary.answered}</strong>
                        <small>Con respuesta registrada</small>
                    </article>

                </section>

                {/* ============================================= */}
                {/* CONTENIDO PRINCIPAL */}
                {/* ============================================= */}

                <section className="member-prayer__content">

                    <div className="member-prayer__section-heading">

                        <div>
                            <span className="member-prayer__section-eyebrow">
                                ACOMPAÑAMIENTO ESPIRITUAL
                            </span>

                            <h2>Unidos en oración</h2>

                            <p>
                                Explora las peticiones compartidas por
                                la comunidad o consulta tus solicitudes.
                            </p>
                        </div>

                        <Heart
                            size={28}
                            className="member-prayer__section-heart"
                            aria-hidden="true"
                        />

                    </div>

                    {/* =========================================== */}
                    {/* PESTAÑAS */}
                    {/* =========================================== */}

                    <div className="member-prayer__tabs">

                        <button
                            type="button"
                            className={`member-prayer__tab ${activeTab === "community"
                                ? "member-prayer__tab--active"
                                : ""
                                }`}
                            onClick={() => setActiveTab("community")}
                        >
                            <Users size={18} aria-hidden="true" />
                            Comunidad
                            <span>{communityRequests.length}</span>
                        </button>

                        <button
                            type="button"
                            className={`member-prayer__tab ${activeTab === "mine"
                                ? "member-prayer__tab--active"
                                : ""
                                }`}
                            onClick={() => setActiveTab("mine")}
                        >
                            <HandHeart size={18} aria-hidden="true" />
                            Mis peticiones
                            <span>{myRequests.length}</span>
                        </button>

                    </div>

                    {/* =========================================== */}
                    {/* FILTROS */}
                    {/* =========================================== */}

                    <div className="member-prayer__filters">

                        <label className="member-prayer__search">
                            <Search size={19} aria-hidden="true" />

                            <input
                                type="search"
                                placeholder="Buscar peticiones..."
                                aria-label="Buscar peticiones"
                                value={search}
                                onChange={(event) =>
                                    setSearch(event.target.value)
                                }
                            />
                        </label>

                        <select
                            aria-label="Filtrar por categoría"
                            value={categoryFilter}
                            onChange={(event) =>
                                setCategoryFilter(event.target.value)
                            }
                        >
                            <option value="all">
                                Todas las categorías
                            </option>

                            {CATEGORIES.map((category) => (
                                <option key={category} value={category}>
                                    {category}
                                </option>
                            ))}
                        </select>

                    </div>

                    {/* =========================================== */}
                    {/* LISTADO */}
                    {/* =========================================== */}

                    <div className="member-prayer__grid">

                        {filteredRequests.map((request) => (
                            <PrayerCard
                                key={request.id}
                                request={request}
                                currentMemberId={currentMemberId}
                                onView={setSelectedRequest}
                            />
                        ))}

                    </div>

                    {filteredRequests.length === 0 && (
                        <div className="member-prayer__empty">
                            <HandHeart size={37} strokeWidth={1.4} />

                            <h3>No encontramos peticiones</h3>

                            <p>
                                Prueba con otros filtros o comparte
                                una nueva petición de oración.
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    )}

                </section>

                {/* ============================================= */}
                {/* NOTA DE PRIVACIDAD */}
                {/* ============================================= */}

                <div className="member-prayer__privacy-note">
                    <ShieldCheck size={20} aria-hidden="true" />

                    <p>
                        Tú decides si tu petición será comunitaria
                        o privada. También puedes ocultar tu nombre
                        a los demás miembros. Los administradores
                        autorizados siempre podrán identificar al
                        solicitante.
                    </p>
                </div>

            </div>

            {/* =============================================== */}
            {/* MODAL NUEVA PETICIÓN */}

                                type="button"
                                className="member-prayer__close"
                                onClick={() => setShowForm(false)}
                                aria-label="Cerrar formulario"

                                <X size={21} />
                            </button>

                                        ? "Miembro anónimo"
                                        : selectedRequest.requester}
                                </span>

                            </p>

                                </span>
                            </div>

                            <button
                                type="button"
                                className="member-prayer__secondary-button"
                                onClick={() => setSelectedRequest(null)}
                            >
                                Cerrar
                            </button>
                        </footer>

                    </section>
                </div>
            )}

        </main>
    );
}

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";

import {

import "../../../styles/pages/super-admin/system/AdministratorsManagement.css";


// =========================================================
// SUCURSALES DE DEMOSTRACIÓN

    sucursalPrincipal: DEMO_BRANCHES[0],
    createdAt: "2026-05-24",
    lastLoginAt: "2026-10-08",
  },
];


// =========================================================
// PÁGINA PRINCIPAL
// =========================================================

function AdministratorsManagement() {

  // =========================================================
  // FILTROS
  // =========================================================

  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [role, setRole] = useState("all");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("all");


  // =========================================================
  // MODAL DE DETALLES
  // =========================================================

  const [selectedAdministrator, setSelectedAdministrator] =
    useState(null);

  const handleViewAdministrator = useCallback((user) => {
    setSelectedAdministrator(user);
  }, []);

  const handleCloseModal = useCallback(() => {
    setSelectedAdministrator(null);
  }, []);


  // =========================================================
  // FILTRAR CUENTAS
  // =========================================================

  const filteredAdministrators = useMemo(() => {

    const normalizedSearch =
      search.trim().toLowerCase();

    return DEMO_ADMINISTRATORS.filter((user) => {

      const fullName =
        `${user.firstName} ${user.lastName}`;

      const branchesText = user.sucursales
        .map((branch) => branch.name)
        .join(" ");

      const matchesSearch =
        !normalizedSearch ||
        [
          fullName,
          user.email,
          branchesText,
        ].some((value) =>
          value.toLowerCase().includes(normalizedSearch)
        );

      const matchesRole =
        role === "all" ||
        user.role === role;

      const matchesStatus =
        status === "all" ||
        (
          status === "active" &&
          user.isActive
        ) ||
        (
          status === "inactive" &&
          !user.isActive
        );

      return (
        matchesSearch &&
        matchesRole &&
        matchesStatus
      );

    });

  }, [search, role, status]);


  // =========================================================
  // LIMPIAR FILTROS
  // =========================================================

  const handleClearFilters = () => {
    setSearch("");
    setRole("all");
    setStatus("all");
  };


  // =========================================================
  // INTERFAZ
  // =========================================================

  return (
    <main className="administrators-management">

      {/* ================================================= */}
      {/* NAVEGACIÓN */}
      {/* ================================================= */}

      <div className="administrators-management__navigation">

        <Link
          to="/super-admin"
          className="administrators-management__back"
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={17} />
          Volver al panel
        </Link>

        <span className="administrators-management__breadcrumb">
          Sistema / Administradores
        </span>

      </div>


      {/* ================================================= */}
      {/* ENCABEZADO */}
      {/* ================================================= */}

      <header className="administrators-management__header">

        <div className="administrators-management__heading">

          <div className="administrators-management__heading-icon">
            <ShieldCheck size={25} />
          </div>

          <div>
            <span className="administrators-management__eyebrow">
              CONTROL DEL SISTEMA
            </span>

            <h1>Administradores</h1>

            <p>
              Consulta las cuentas con privilegios
              administrativos y sus sucursales autorizadas.
            </p>
          </div>

        </div>

        <span className="administrators-management__access">
          <LockKeyhole size={14} />
          Solo superadministrador
        </span>

      </header>


      {/* ================================================= */}
      {/* AVISO DE DEMOSTRACIÓN */}
      {/* ================================================= */}

      <div className="administrators-management__demo">
        <Info size={18} />

        <p>
          <strong>Modo demostración:</strong>{" "}
          las cuentas mostradas son ficticias.
          Puedes utilizar los filtros y consultar
          sus detalles sin modificar MongoDB.
        </p>
      </div>


      {/* ================================================= */}
      {/* ESTADÍSTICAS */}
      {/* ================================================= */}

      <AdministratorsSummary
        administrators={DEMO_ADMINISTRATORS}
      />


      {/* ================================================= */}
      {/* LISTADO */}
      {/* ================================================= */}

      <section className="administrators-management__panel">

        <div className="administrators-management__panel-header">

          <div>
            <h2>
              <UsersRound size={19} />
              Cuentas administrativas
            </h2>

            <p>
              Listado de administradores y
              superadministradores registrados.
            </p>
          </div>

          <span className="administrators-management__count">
            {filteredAdministrators.length} resultados
          </span>

        </div>


        <AdministratorsFilters
          search={search}
          role={role}
          status={status}
          onSearchChange={setSearch}
          onRoleChange={setRole}
          onStatusChange={setStatus}
          onClear={handleClearFilters}
        />


        <AdministratorsTable
          administrators={filteredAdministrators}
          onView={handleViewAdministrator}
        />

      </section>


      {/* ================================================= */}
      {/* INFORMACIÓN DE SEGURIDAD */}
      {/* ================================================= */}

      <aside className="administrators-management__security">

        <LockKeyhole size={20} />

        <div>
          <strong>Protección de cuentas privilegiadas</strong>

          <p>
            Los cambios de rol, la desactivación de
            administradores y las modificaciones de
            cuentas superadministradoras requerirán
            autorización del backend y registro de
            auditoría. Esta versión es solo de consulta.
          </p>
        </div>

      </aside>


      {/* ================================================= */}
      {/* MODAL */}
      {/* ================================================= */}

      {selectedAdministrator && (

        <AdministratorDetailsModal
          administrator={selectedAdministrator}
          onClose={handleCloseModal}
        />

      )}

    </main>
  );
}

export default AdministratorsManagement;

import { Route } from "react-router-dom";

// =========================================================
// PÁGINAS ADMINISTRATIVAS COMPARTIDAS
// =========================================================

import UsersManagement from "../pages/administration/UsersManagement";

import RegisterUser from "../pages/administration/RegisterUser";

import ActivityHistory from "../pages/administration/ActivityHistory";

import EventsManagement from "../pages/administration/EventsManagement";

import PrayerRequestsManagement from "../pages/administration/PrayerRequestsManagement";

import MembersManagement from "../pages/administration/MembersManagement";

// =========================================================
// DEFINICIÓN CENTRALIZADA DE RUTAS
// =========================================================

// Este archivo contiene las rutas que serán
// utilizadas por admin y super_admin.
//
// La autenticación y autorización de acceso
// a cada panel se realizan desde App.js.
//
// Los permisos específicos de cada operación
// deberán validarse también en el backend.

function SharedAdminRoutes() {
    return (
        <>
            {/* Gestión de usuarios */}
            <Route
                path="sistema/usuarios"
                element={<UsersManagement />}
            />

            {/* Registro de usuarios */}
            <Route
                path="sistema/registrar-usuario"
                element={<RegisterUser />}
            />

            <Route
                path="historial"
                element={<ActivityHistory />}
            />

            <Route
                path="eventos"
                element={<EventsManagement />}
            />

            <Route
                path="peticiones-oracion"
                element={<PrayerRequestsManagement />}
            />

            <Route
                path="miembros"
                element={<MembersManagement />}
            />

            {/* Próximos módulos compartidos:
          - Eventos.
          - Peticiones de oración.
          - Miembros.
          - Recursos.
          - Gestión de páginas.
      */}
        </>
    );
}

export default SharedAdminRoutes;




File: C:\Users\Liss\Desktop\iglesia-el-ancla\frontend\src\services\api.js

// Importamos Axios para realizar
// solicitudes HTTP al backend.
import axios from "axios";


// Creamos una instancia reutilizable
// para toda la aplicación.
const api = axios.create({
  // Obtenemos la URL desde el .env del frontend.
  //
  // Si no existe, utilizamos el backend local.
  baseURL:
    process.env.REACT_APP_API_URL ||
    "http://localhost:5000/api",

  // Permitimos que el navegador envíe
  // y reciba cookies HttpOnly.
  withCredentials: true,

  // Definimos un tiempo máximo
  // de espera de diez segundos.
  timeout: 10000,

  // Indicamos que enviaremos
  // y recibiremos información JSON.
  headers: {
    "Content-Type": "application/json",
    Accept: "application/json",
  },
});


// Exportamos la instancia para utilizarla
// en login, gastos, eventos y demás módulos.
export default api;



File: C:\Users\Liss\Desktop\iglesia-el-ancla\scan_project.js

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const excludedFolders = ['node_modules', 'imagenes', 'public', 'assets', '.git', 'build'];
const excludedExtensions = ['.jpg', '.png', '.css'];
const excludedFiles = ['package-lock.json', 'README.md', '.gitignore', '.gitattributes'];
const outputFileName = 'files_list.txt';

function scanDirectory(directory) {
    let filesContent = '';

    const files = fs.readdirSync(directory);

    files.forEach(file => {
        const filePath = path.join(directory, file);
        const stat = fs.statSync(filePath);

        if (stat.isDirectory() && !excludedFolders.includes(file)) {
            filesContent += scanDirectory(filePath);
        } else if (stat.isFile()) {
            const ext = path.extname(file);
            if (!excludedExtensions.includes(ext) && !excludedFiles.includes(file)) {
                const fileContent = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');
                filesContent += `\n\nFile: ${filePath}\n\n${fileContent}\n\n`;
            }
        }
    });

    return filesContent;
}

function writeToFile(content) {
    fs.writeFileSync(outputFileName, content, 'utf8');
    console.log(`File content written to ${outputFileName}`);
}

const filesContent = scanDirectory(__dirname);
writeToFile(filesContent);
